{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Build a Python script at /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/tools/generate-canvas.py that generates Obsidian .canvas files from the Mardov vault data.\n\nThe vault is at /Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/\n\nObsidian .canvas format is JSON:\n```json\n{\n  \"nodes\": [\n    {\"id\": \"uuid\", \"type\": \"file\", \"file\": \"path/to/note.md\", \"x\": 0, \"y\": 0, \"width\": 250, \"height\": 60, \"color\": \"1\"},\n    {\"id\": \"uuid\", \"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Some label\", \"x\": 100, \"y\": 100, \"width\": 200, \"height\": 50}\n  ],\n  \"edges\": [\n    {\"id\": \"uuid\", \"fromNode\": \"id1\", \"toNode\": \"id2\", \"label\": \"relationship\"}\n  ]\n}\n```\n\nColors: \"1\" red, \"2\" orange, \"3\" yellow, \"4\" green, \"5\" cyan, \"6\" purple\n\nThe script should generate THREE canvas files:\n\n### 1. `mardov-vault/canvas/relationship-map.canvas`\n- Read character files from `mardov-vault/characters/` (both pcs/ and npcs/ subdirs)\n- Read `mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/character-relationships.md` for relationship data\n- Place PCs in a central ring, NPCs arranged around them by faction/affiliation\n- Color code: PCs green, major NPCs orange, minor NPCs yellow, antagonists red, deities purple\n- Draw edges for known relationships (allies, enemies, family, romantic, etc.)\n- Use force-directed-like layout: PCs in center circle, NPCs radiate outward\n\n### 2. `mardov-vault/canvas/timeline.canvas`\n- Read `mardov-vault/lore/timeline.md` and the timeline extraction files\n- Read chapter files for key events\n- Arrange events left-to-right chronologically\n- Color by Act: Act 1 green, Act 2 yellow, Act 3 orange, Act 4 red, Act 5 purple\n- Group vertically by story thread if possible\n\n### 3. `mardov-vault/canvas/faction-web.canvas`\n- Read faction files from `mardov-vault/factions/`\n- Read character files for faction membership\n- Place factions as large nodes, members as smaller nodes around them\n- Color factions by alignment/disposition\n- Draw edges for inter-faction relationships (allied, hostile, neutral)\n\nRequirements:\n- Create `mardov-vault/canvas/` directory if needed\n- Parse the actual vault files to extract data — don't hardcode\n- Handle missing data gracefully (some files may be stubs)\n- Generate unique IDs for nodes/edges\n- Print stats about what was generated\n\nRead the actual vault files to understand the data structure before building."},"uuid":"eebc7439-7e53-410b-ac90-22a577758067","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:10.831Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"1dc14e44-8fd1-4613-aea9-e2a7f28df13c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Unfeu9WfwMp664SpafVRNP","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"character-relationships\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/character-relationships.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign -- Character Relationship Map\n     8→\n     9→Generated: 2026-03-17\n    10→Sources: Player Summaries 0-46, Character Backstories, Chapter Notes 5-136, Character Motivations, Prophesies & Zeniths, Pilot Story Bible\n    11→\n    12→---\n    13→\n    14→## Table of Contents\n    15→\n    16→1. [Core Party Roster](#roster)\n    17→2. [Part 1: Relationship Grid](#part1)\n    18→3. [Part 2: Character Arcs Through Relationships](#part2)\n    19→4. [Part 3: Production-Critical Dynamics for the Pilot](#part3)\n    20→\n    21→---\n    22→\n    23→<a name=\"roster\"></a>\n    24→\n    25→## Core Party Roster\n    26→\n    27→The Scarlet Tunic Brigade / The Awakened -- 13+ PCs rotating in and out across 136 chapters. Core party members by presence and narrative weight:\n    28→\n    29→| Tier | Character | Race/Class | Player Archetype | Chapters Active |\n    30→|------|-----------|-----------|-----------------|-----------------|\n    31→| Core | Brigit Wildfire | Half-Elf Druid | The Heart, the chronicler | All (author of 109 chapter notes) |\n    32→| Core | Daina (Diesa) Fireforge | Hill Dwarf Barbarian | The Brawler, the force of nature | All |\n    33→| Core | Stalwart Little Goldentooth | Dragonborn Paladin | The Anchor, self-appointed leader | All |\n    34→| Core | Nyx Khamestria | Tiefling Warlock | The Wildcard, the outsider | All |\n    35→| Core | Sprocket Overspark | Gnome Wizard (Divination) | The Brain, the fatalist | All |\n    36→| Core | Nocturnus Wayne | Drow Monk | The Loner, the dark knight | All |\n    37→| Core | Randal | Bard/Warlock | The Mediator, the scholar | Ch. 10+ |\n    38→| Core | Tezka (Tesca) Duskhallow | Tiefling Monk | The Scrapper, the survivor | Ch. 10+ |\n    39→| Major | Bergs (Barnabus) Fireforge | Hill Dwarf Fighter | The Wild Man, the comedic tank | All |\n    40→| Major | Varis Liadon | Half-Elf Ranger | The Outsider (literally from another plane) | Intermittent |\n    41→| Major | Tiberius D. Rex | Dragonborn Cleric | The Pious Comic Relief | Ch. 8-32 |\n    42→| Major | Irethia | Draconic Sorcerer | The Enigma, the secret-keeper | Ch. 16+ |\n    43→| Minor | Dallor Dallorbill | Human Rogue | The Turncoat, the greedy survivor | Ch. 0-25 |\n    44→| Minor | Fen Darkeyes (BrightHallow) | Dragonborn Ranger | The Cursed One | Mid-campaign |\n    45→\n    46→---\n    47→\n    48→<a name=\"part1\"></a>\n    49→\n    50→## Part 1: Relationship Grid\n    51→\n    52→### Brigit & Daina -- \"Sisters in Arms\"\n    53→\n    54→- **How they met**: Both members of the original Scarlet Tunic Brigade intake (Ch. 0). From the very first encounter in the sewers beneath Argos, they fought side by side.\n    55→- **Core dynamic**: Battle sisters. Brigit is cerebral and cautious; Daina is impulsive and violent. They complement each other perfectly -- Brigit plans, Daina executes. Together they are the party's most reliable combat pairing.\n    56→- **Key bonding moment**: Brigit repeatedly notes Daina's actions with a mix of exasperation and admiration. In Summary 29, Brigit writes about turning into a giant eagle specifically to \"run interference\" and be \"a safety net for these guys. We've been through way too much together.\" That \"these guys\" is principally Daina, who frequently leaps before looking (jumping off a 50ft cliff in Ch. 27, charging a T-Rex face-first in Ch. 23).\n    57→- **Key conflict moment**: The soul-harvesting debate (Ch. 19). Daina is willing to extract souls from a soul bag for practical purposes (disguises for Nyx and Tezka). Brigit finds this morally repugnant. Brigit writes: \"Daina's against it, but Nyx and Tezka super need it.\" This moral tension -- Daina's pragmatism vs. Brigit's nature-reverence -- recurs but never breaks them.\n    58→- **Running gag**: Brigit's notes religiously track \"the total Daina hitting Dallor count\" (5 in one session alone during Ch. 7).\n    59→- **Late campaign**: Daina undergoes a \"rebirth\" (platinum hair, glowing runic armor, warhammer) and Brigit achieves \"Progenitor Form\" (dark elven figure wreathed in fire). Both have transformed beyond recognition from the scruffy recruit and wild druid of Chapter 0, but their bond is the campaign's bedrock.\n    60→\n    61→---\n    62→\n    63→### Brigit & Sprocket -- \"The Odd Couple\"\n    64→\n    65→- **How they met**: Original Brigade intake. Sprocket's mage hand touched the Demi-Lich phylactery in Ch. 2, and Brigit helped crack it open -- their first combined act.\n    66→- **Core dynamic**: Big sister / little brother. Brigit is exasperated by Sprocket's recklessness (\"recklessly he charged directly into caltrops\" -- Ch. 25) but deeply protective. Sprocket is the party's strongest caster and Brigit is its strongest healer, making them the magical backbone.\n    67→- **Key bonding moment**: During Summary 29, Brigit, Irethia, and Varis take pity on Sprocket and let him sleep in their room. \"Daina can be quite intimidating for such a small creature.\" This small domestic kindness reveals Brigit's maternal instinct toward the gnome.\n    68→- **Key conflict moment**: Sprocket tries to abandon the tieflings in Ch. 19 -- \"Sprocket says leave the tieflings here, and we should move on.\" Brigit is horrified. This is a rare moment where the party's smartest member reveals cold pragmatism that puts him at odds with Brigit's moral compass.\n    69→- **Prophetic bond**: Both are assigned to the Famine Zenith room (Harvest Room) alongside Varis. The elk chose Brigit, the rabbit chose Sprocket. Their fates are cosmically intertwined.\n    70→- **Late campaign**: Sprocket's divination magic and Brigit's druidic powers become two sides of the same coin -- fate and nature. Sprocket knows he is marked for death (\"the ultimate answer to a question you ask yourself every fight\") and Brigit is his emotional anchor.\n    71→\n    72→---\n    73→\n    74→### Brigit & Nyx -- \"Fire and Darkness\"\n    75→\n    76→- **How they met**: Original Brigade intake.\n    77→- **Core dynamic**: Reluctant allies who become deeply loyal friends. Nyx is a warlock who traffics in darkness and pacts; Brigit is a druid who channels fire and nature. They shouldn't work together, but they do.\n    78→- **Key bonding moment**: The Vodram mission (Ch. 28). Brigit, Nyx, Irethia, and Varis are transported to Vecna's domain. Brigit transforms into mice with Irethia while Nyx makes them invisible. Varis writes: \"Nyx (and Brigit, and Irethia) came through in the battle... Nyx somehow (in the middle of battle!!?!?!) convinced this guy to toss his super scary darkness sword away.\" Their teamwork in extreme danger forges real trust.\n    79→- **Key conflict moment**: Early campaign -- Nyx lights braziers impulsively (Ch. 3-4), causing undead swarms. Brigit's notes frequently convey frustration with Nyx's shoot-first approach.\n    80→- **Pivotal scene**: Nyx destroys the Eye of Vecna's head with twin fire beams (Ch. 28) while Brigit pins the enemy down in wolf form. This is their definitive \"we work together\" moment.\n    81→- **Late campaign**: Nyx's final summary (Ch. 46) reveals deep introspection about trust, patrons, and the amulet. She addresses it to the party with the sign-off \"I hope you can forgive me.\" Brigit is among those she hopes will understand.\n    82→\n    83→---\n    84→\n    85→### Brigit & Varis -- \"The Forest Bond\"\n    86→\n    87→- **How they met**: Varis appears through a portal in Ch. 6 as a member of the Scarlet Tunic Brigade from an alternate reality (Vodram, the world destroyed by Vecna).\n    88→- **Core dynamic**: Fellow protectors of the wild. Varis is a ranger dedicated to the Doe; Brigit is a druid chosen by the Stag. They share a cosmological bond neither fully understands at first. Both are at home in forests and uncomfortable in cities.\n    89→- **Key bonding moment**: Summary 28 (Varis's travelogue). Varis writes about Brigit, Nyx, and Irethia with genuine awe and gratitude: \"These people from Sabine's world... they got dropped or pushed into the worst place possible, and never complained, just asked 'How do we help?'\" He specifically notes Brigit seeing something on his face he \"hopes is never directed at me\" -- she saw him kill Roderick in cold blood, and still chose to trust him. \"Still amazed they trust me this much.\"\n    90→- **Key conflict moment**: None direct between them. They are the campaign's most harmonious pair. However, Varis's willingness to execute people (Roderick) sits uneasily with Brigit's druidic worldview.\n    91→- **Prophetic bond**: Both assigned to the same Zenith room (Forest/Famine). Elk chose Brigit, Doe chose Varis. The doe and elk \"stand side by side above a forest of mangrove trees\" in the hieroglyphics.\n    92→- **Late campaign**: They secretly collaborate -- sneaking off together to find the hobgoblin Grinda (Ch. 29), with Brigit covering for Varis in wolf form while he navigates with hidden intelligence sources.\n    93→\n    94→---\n    95→\n    96→### Stalwart & Dallor -- \"The Grudge\"\n    97→\n    98→- **How they met**: Original Brigade intake.\n    99→- **Core dynamic**: Stalwart is the party's self-appointed moral arbiter. Dallor is the party's self-appointed opportunist. They are natural enemies. Stalwart views Dallor as a traitor; Dallor views Stalwart as a bully.\n   100→- **Key bonding moment**: There isn't one. This is a deliberately antagonistic relationship.\n   101→- **Key conflict moment**: The Betrayal (Ch. 2). Dallor Dallorbill \"cuts a deal with Mordred to save himself from being murdered.\" The party never forgives him. In Ch. 3, \"Stalwart Little, Nyx, Nocturnus Wayne and the accused turn clock, Dallor Dallorbill\" confront him. \"Dallor was quick to plead for his life... Those were Dallor's last words as he was quickly knocked unconscious by his former party.\" Only Brotor stabilizes him.\n   102→- **Running gag**: Stalwart physically abuses Dallor constantly. In Ch. 6, the \"light vs. dark\" competition pairs \"Stalwart vs Dallor: catch something out of Dallor's hand. Stalwart punches him.\" Daina also hits Dallor (5 times in one session).\n   103→- **Nocturnus on Dallor** (Summary 23): \"How he stays living is a wonder. If he betrays us, I'll get the pleasure of disposing of him. Again.\"\n   104→- **Where it stands**: Dallor eventually fades from the campaign, but the dynamic establishes a crucial party norm: betrayal is not forgiven.\n   105→\n   106→---\n   107→\n   108→### Stalwart & Nocturnus -- \"The Odd Couple of Darkness\"\n   109→\n   110→- **How they met**: Original Brigade intake.\n   111→- **Core dynamic**: Two loners who respect each other's competence but fundamentally disagree about methods. Stalwart is a paladin of Bahamut -- order, justice, honor. Nocturnus is a drow monk obsessed with vengeance against his aunt Oblivion Wayne. Both are \"dark knight\" archetypes but from opposite moral traditions.\n   112→- **Key bonding moment**: They consistently watch each other's backs in combat. Nocturnus uses Slow Fall to save Daina when Stalwart can't reach her (Ch. 27). Despite Nocturnus's \"I save these fools\" attitude, he repeatedly acts to protect Stalwart.\n   113→- **Key conflict moment**: The Nightmare Sword arc. Nocturnus acquires Nightmare, a sentient evil sword, and begins killing hags the party was trying to save (Ch. 38). \"Bergs being Bergs grabs the cauldron... our resident evildoer decides to kill the evil hags. We tried to stop him, but he won't listen.\" Brigit tries to polymorph Nocturnus into a bunny. Sylvia stuns him. The party literally has to restrain him.\n   114→- **Where it stands**: The party forces Nocturnus into restrictions (\"only allowed to fight non-living things\"). The mushroom Sovereign gives him a \"pacifying spore\" that covers half his face. This is a functional relationship between light and dark -- the party needs Nocturnus's power but fears his corruption.\n   115→\n   116→---\n   117→\n   118→### Stalwart & Brigit -- \"The Paladin and the Druid\"\n   119→\n   120→- **How they met**: Original Brigade intake.\n   121→- **Core dynamic**: Mutual respect between two protectors. Stalwart protects through force and faith; Brigit protects through nature and cunning. Stalwart is loud, direct, and occasionally brutal (carving messages into a hobgoblin's chest -- Ch. 24). Brigit is the quiet observer who chronicles everything.\n   122→- **Key bonding moment**: Stalwart consistently casts Shield of Faith on party members in danger. In the T-Rex fight (Ch. 23), \"Stalwart casts Shield of Faith on Daina which is just in time, because the T-Rex bites down on Daina.\" This protective instinct extends to everyone.\n   123→- **Key conflict**: Stalwart's brutality toward prisoners disturbs Brigit but she never directly confronts him. Her notes shift from \"oh well actually hunt other people\" to acceptance. She recognizes he channels his rage productively -- toward goblins (avenging his brother Stewart's death at the Sunless Citadel).\n   124→- **Prophetic divergence**: They are in different Zenith groups. Stalwart is in Pestilence (Bynar) while Brigit is in Famine/Forest (Macchu). Their paths will eventually diverge geographically.\n   125→\n   126→---\n   127→\n   128→### Daina & Bergs -- \"The Fireforge Connection\"\n   129→\n   130→- **How they met**: Both bear the Fireforge surname. Bergs was planted by the Artificer's Guild among the Scarlet Tunic Brigade as an agent. Their shared surname suggests a familial bond -- possibly cousins or distant relatives from the same dwarven clan.\n   131→- **Core dynamic**: Family recognition happening in slow motion. Daina doesn't know her own heritage (Thorkon Fireforge's daughter, secret Bronzebeard connection). Bergs may know more than he lets on. Both are drawn to dwarven matters -- the forges, the Magna Har, the Fireforged weapons.\n   132→- **Key bonding moment**: The Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc (Ch. 37-38). Both are identified as Fireforges by Ifrin the Pactbound. \"Bartrim says he's a Forgesworn... He mentions the other Fireforges in the group.\" Both survive the phoenix's eruption when the non-Fireforges are incinerated by lava.\n   133→- **Key conflict moment**: Bergs trades his maul to Ifrin without consulting Daina. This is his weapon and his choice, but it affects the entire group's dwarven destiny.\n   134→- **Character Motivation doc**: \"Bergs has discovered that despite being abandoned by his parents and raised by wolves, somehow he's been roped into this strange Maghar Dwarven mumbo-jumbo.\"\n   135→- **Where it stands**: Both are apprentice Magna Har (Daina the axe, Bergs the hammer). Their parallel arcs toward discovering their Fireforge heritage is one of the campaign's major slow-burn revelations. Daina needs to find Decan Smithrunner; Bergs needs to find his mother, the master hammersmith.\n   136→\n   137→---\n   138→\n   139→### Nyx & Tezka -- \"The Tiefling Sisters\"\n   140→\n   141→- **How they met**: Both join around Ch. 10. Both are tieflings in a world that hangs tieflings from trees for practicing magic. Instant kinship.\n   142→- **Core dynamic**: Survivors of prejudice. Tezka was raised in a monastery that treated her as an outcast. Nyx carries the weight of her sister Xyn being trapped in a demonic pact. Both need soul-based disguises to move through the Magister's Circle without being murdered. This shared vulnerability creates a bond deeper than friendship.\n   143→- **Key bonding moment**: The disguise arc (Ch. 19). Both Nyx and Tezka must accept soul-glamours to survive. Nyx gets the Revenant's soul (constantly screaming \"REVENGE. KILL THE GNOME.\" in her head). Tezka uses a chicken's soul. They share the burden of constantly maintaining false identities.\n   144→- **Key conflict moment**: Tezka burns off her mushroom hat successfully (Ch. 29), but the same approach is more traumatic for others. Their different responses to magical coercion highlight Tezka's physical resilience vs. Nyx's psychological fragility.\n   145→- **Prophetic bond**: Both assigned to the Harvest Zenith room alongside Irethia. Fox, Owl, Mouse. Their destinies are linked to Dawnpass and Tygras Logar.\n   146→- **Late campaign**: Nyx's Summary 46 reveals the depth of her internal struggle with patrons and power. Tezka's motivations (from the Character Motivations doc) are about justice: \"An enemy is just a friend you've yet to understand.\" They represent two responses to oppression -- Nyx through cunning and pacts, Tezka through discipline and patience.\n   147→\n   148→---\n   149→\n   150→### Sprocket & Bergs -- \"The Buddy Cop Duo\""}]},"uuid":"e1bf09a0-7af5-499b-8b8b-51bd3d03464a","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:21.601Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1dc14e44-8fd1-4613-aea9-e2a7f28df13c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"0dd2ac38-ee47-4a69-b000-92ad613abada","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BnaZpBQYHfVLzRsNdCF9BA","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Master Timeline\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Master Timeline of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## Ancient History\n     9→\n    10→- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] — original twin souls, divine jealousy begins\n    11→- [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] founds Circle of the Murder ([[Mordred|Ramses]]) + [[Artificer's Guild]] ([[Xerxes]])\n    12→- [[Mordred|Ramses]] forces lichdom on [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]; she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[Uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[Forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[Forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. Sewer descent. |\n    47→| 1 | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] pickpockets note from [[Jorgen]] — proof of setup. |\n    48→| 2 | [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]]. \"2,000 souls.\" [[Jorgen]] killed, becomes revenant. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. Soul tether. |\n    50→| 6 | Dopplegangers. [[V-Mardov]] rift opens. [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from alternate reality. |\n    51→| 7-8 | [[Jorgen]] revenant pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]]. [[Thornberry Shade]] ship. [[Jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[MIA]]). [[MIA]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[Jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Tiefling prejudice arc. |\n    62→| 19 | Soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]. |\n    63→| 23 | Zodiac spirits revealed — they bet their immortality on the champions. |\n    64→| 24-25 | Tomb of the Last Curator. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed. [[Xyn]] used as messenger. |\n    68→| 34 | [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter discovered inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe. [[Forgesworn]] lore revealed. [[Nocturnus Wayne]] kills cousin Dusken, becomes Drider King. |\n    69→| 36 | [[Fen Darkeyes|Devin Darkeyes]] found enslaved by the Aboleth. |\n    70→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc. [[Forgesworn]] connections revealed. |\n    71→\n    72→### Act 3 (Chapters 41-86) — The Escalation\n    73→\n    74→| Ch. | Event |\n    75→|-----|-------|\n    76→| 42+ | Campaign splits into parallel tracks. |\n    77→| ~66 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]]'s Homunculus Thraalm killed. |\n    78→| ~82 | Attempted revival of [[Zodiac|Falak]] the serpent using captured Mahen. |\n    79→| 86 | Reunion. Battle of the Distillery. |\n    80→\n    81→### Act 4 (Chapters 87-136) — The Endgame\n    82→\n    83→| Ch. | Event |\n    84→|-----|-------|\n    85→| 103 | Second Reunion. TPK and revival via myconid network. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] carrying Kelemtor. |\n    86→| 104-105 | [[Reginald Providence]] killed (kenku in disguise). [[Anchor of War]] silenced. Therzadun thwarted. Silence/Timothy unmasked as Therzadun's vessel. |\n    87→| 106 | Declaration of War on gods. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar created in image of [[Ashara Everstar]]. |\n    89→| 108 | Three races of elves' gods revealed. Casaval reveals [[Irethia]] (prototype), [[Riven Fen|Fen]] (warden), [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] (bane). [[Lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    90→| 109 | [[Josh Coolbreeze]] cleanses [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] of vampirism. [[Lolth]]-[[Raven Queen]] deal revealed. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[Rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. Hearthstone exposed as triple agent. |\n    95→| 135 | Book of Shadows plot to open Harvest and Winter Zenith. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→"}]},"uuid":"5da01762-2b02-4c6f-9179-afd7c0cad5d1","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:22.391Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0dd2ac38-ee47-4a69-b000-92ad613abada","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"3c6d0ec7-e7ad-432d-8d56-abb4fa82aea3","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01Dk5KENLZL6a2nxyKD6M9Py","toolUseID":"toolu_01Dk5KENLZL6a2nxyKD6M9Py","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:23.345Z","uuid":"943cee49-77c1-4af0-96d7-497ec260fd3d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"5f20fea7-90e3-4dab-b2ff-2695ed6bf8fb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Dk5KENLZL6a2nxyKD6M9Py","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Brigit Wildfire\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Wild Spirit of Fire\", \"The Heart\"]\n     6→race: \"Changeling/Shapeshifter\"\n     7→class: \"Druid (Circle of Wildfire) / Barbarian multiclass\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[The Distillery]], [[Good Guys Guild]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Liz, Ch. 0-136)\", \"Backstories\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Brigit/Brigite Wildfire.jpg\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Brigit/Brigit's Progenitor Form.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Brigit Wildfire is the emotional center of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] and the campaign's primary narrator, having authored 109 of the party's 176 chapter notes. She is a druid of wildfire renewal who channels the ancient power of the [[Stag Zodiac]], chosen vessel of the Progenitors, and custodian of the [[Anchor of Death]]. More than any other party member, Brigit is the one who remembers, who documents, and who refuses to let any moment be forgotten.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→### Normal Form\n    23→A young woman with vibrant red hair, partially shaved on one side, swept back and falling in loose waves. She has bright green eyes, fair skin dusted with freckles across her cheeks and nose, and slightly pointed ears hinting at her changeling nature. Her expression in repose is watchful and intelligent, lips slightly parted as though mid-thought.\n    24→\n    25→She wears a **green bodice** with decorative stitching along the neckline, laced up the front with brown leather cord over a darker undershirt. A **dark cloak** drapes over one shoulder, clasped at the throat with a simple metal pin. Her forearms are wrapped in **tan leather bracers** with visible stitching. A sturdy **leather belt** with a metal buckle sits at her waist. Her overall palette is forest greens, warm browns, and dark earth tones -- the colors of someone who lives closer to the woods than to any city.\n    26→\n    27→Her most striking visual feature is the **glowing molten fire orb** she conjures above her open palm -- a crackling sphere of orange-red magma and embers that illuminates her face from above, casting warm light across her freckled skin and catching the green of her eyes. This is her wildfire magic made manifest, and she holds it with casual familiarity.\n    28→\n    29→### Progenitor Form\n    30→A dramatic transformation into something ancient and otherworldly. In this form, Brigit appears as a **dark ethereal elven woman** with wild, flowing dark red-brown hair whipping upward as though caught in an invisible storm. Her ears are sharply pointed, her skin has shifted to a pale grey-blue tone, and her eyes blaze with pure glowing white light -- no pupils, no iris, only radiance.\n    31→\n    32→She wears **dark ornate armor** with metallic chains draped across her chest and shoulders. The most striking element is the **glowing red-orange energy** that cracks through her skin and armor like magma breaking through cooling stone -- luminous fissures running along her shoulders, arms, and chest. Embers and sparks trail from these cracks, drifting upward into darkness. A small amber pendant or jewel hangs from her neck. The overall effect is terrifying and beautiful: the freckled druid girl is gone, replaced by something primal, volcanic, and ancient.\n    33→\n    34→### Visual Evolution\n    35→Brigit's appearance charts her arc from uncertain young druid to Progenitor. Early campaign Brigit is the green-clad girl with the fire orb -- warm, approachable, grounded. Late campaign Brigit in Progenitor form is cold, alien, and wreathed in cracking light. The two forms share red hair and fierce eyes, but little else. This contrast is the visual story of the character.\n    36→\n    37→## Personality\n    38→\n    39→**ENFJ (The Protagonist)** -- Brigit is the emotional center of gravity. She organizes, documents, worries, and rallies. She leads not through authority but through sheer force of caring.\n    40→\n    41→**Motivations**: Protect her found family above all else. Understand and master the wild forces inside her -- the Stag, the Progenitor form, the zodiac connections. Secretly, she is terrified that the fire inside her is not renewal but destruction.\n    42→\n    43→**Coping mechanisms**: Brigit copes by doing. She writes notes obsessively, makes plans, skyrites warnings over towns. She processes emotion through transformation -- literally shifting into animals when overwhelmed. Her humor is dry, exasperated, and grounded. She is the \"mom friend\" who hides exhaustion behind competence.\n    44→\n    45→**Under pressure**: Frighteningly focused. The warmth drops out of her voice. She stops writing notes and starts giving orders. Embers trail from her hair. At peak stress she shifts into Lox or the Progenitor -- when she stops being Brigit entirely and becomes something ancient and terrifying.\n    46→\n    47→**Moral compass**: Will always protect innocents. Will never abandon a party member. Frames morality through renewal: fire that burns to let new growth emerge. She would burn a forest to save the people inside it.\n    48→\n    49→## Backstory\n    50→\n    51→Brigit was an orphaned druid from the wilds outside [[Windhaven]]. Her parents were killed by fearful villagers who did not understand her nature. She grew up trusting animals more than people, living on the edges of civilization. When the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] put out a call for recruits, she answered -- arriving at the town walls in wolf form before shifting to human, establishing her duality from the very first moment.\n    52→\n    53→## Key Moments\n    54→\n    55→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    56→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    57→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by [[Sabine Ra]] | Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    58→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | Party cohesion building |\n    59→| 13 | Liberation of [[Argos]] | First major quest completion |\n    60→| 28 | Vodram mission with [[Nyx]], [[Irethia]], [[Varis]] | Transforms into mice with Irethia; forges deep trust with the sub-group |\n    61→| 30-37 | Underdark descent | Party faces darkness together |\n    62→| 86 | The Reunion | Becomes a battlefield commander coordinating scattered party members across a continent |\n    63→| 100 | Shadowfell | Grapples with versions of herself that died or never existed |\n    64→| 119-125 | Time fractures | Confronts the revelation that she IS a Progenitor -- not just channeling one |\n    65→| 130 | Progenitor ritual in Eden with the [[Children of the Forest]] | Ascends the locket, gains custody of the [[Anchor of Death]] |\n    66→| 136 | Final battle | Loses her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle; must find her way forward |\n    67→\n    68→## Relationships\n    69→\n    70→- **[[Daina Fireforge]]** -- Battle sisters. Brigit is cerebral and cautious; Daina is impulsive and violent. They complement each other perfectly. Brigit plans, Daina executes. The campaign's bedrock relationship.\n    71→- **[[Sprocket Overspark]]** -- Big sister / little brother energy. Brigit is exasperated by Sprocket's recklessness but deeply protective. They are the magical backbone of the party. Both assigned to the [[Forest Zenith]] (Elk and Rabbit).\n    72→- **[[Nyx Khamestria]]** -- Fire and Darkness. Reluctant allies who became deeply loyal friends. A warlock of darkness and a druid of fire who shouldn't work together, but do.\n    73→- **[[Varis Laindon]]** -- The Forest Bond. Fellow protectors of the wild, cosmologically linked through the [[Stag Zodiac]] and [[Doe Zodiac]]. The campaign's most harmonious pairing.\n    74→- **[[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]** -- Mutual respect between two protectors. Brigit's notes shift from discomfort with Stalwart's brutality to acceptance that he channels rage productively.\n    75→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- The party fought to save Nocturnus from the [[Nightmare Sword]] rather than abandon him. Brigit tried to polymorph him into a bunny. Uses Greater Restoration on him daily.\n    76→\n    77→## Fighting Style\n    78→\n    79→**Class**: Druid (Circle of Wildfire), high level with access to 7th+ level spells.\n    80→"}]},"uuid":"7d554962-21c7-4461-ac0f-ee1e2b5031fd","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:23.345Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5f20fea7-90e3-4dab-b2ff-2695ed6bf8fb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"67632c70-c10d-46b1-b7be-d5b65006a871","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01YKpyoNu7W9xRenTbtUHRzZ","toolUseID":"toolu_01YKpyoNu7W9xRenTbtUHRzZ","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:23.785Z","uuid":"f3278eaf-4fcd-4bdb-93eb-bf4a292527c8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"f3278eaf-4fcd-4bdb-93eb-bf4a292527c8","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01YKpyoNu7W9xRenTbtUHRzZ","toolUseID":"toolu_01YKpyoNu7W9xRenTbtUHRzZ","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:23.785Z","uuid":"0d1ed08a-01c4-4473-9fc1-c5cf580efcbf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"67632c70-c10d-46b1-b7be-d5b65006a871","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YKpyoNu7W9xRenTbtUHRzZ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Vecna Sworn\"\n     4→aliases: [\"Vecna's Four Zeniths\", \"The Zenith Operations\"]\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"[[Vecna]]\"\n     7→headquarters: \"[[V-Mardov]]\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Vecna Sworn\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→[[Vecna]]'s conquest of [[Mardov]] is organized through four domains of power — the **Four Zeniths**. Each Zenith has a lieutenant, a target, and a nested web of agents. [[Vecna]] rules [[V-Mardov]] (a parallel dimension mirroring the material plane) and commands all four operations.\n    17→\n    18→**Ultimate Goal:** Destroy all keystones, enable [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion of the material plane.\n    19→\n    20→## The Four Zenith Operations\n    21→\n    22→### Zenith of Death (Forest's Zenith — Machu)\n    23→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Wilde]] (Emerald)\n    24→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Death]] (obsidian sword)\n    25→- **Eye:** Green Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    26→- **Primary Agent:** [[Mordred]] / [[Mordred|Ramses]]\n    27→  - [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] (soul-harvesting front)\n    28→  - [[Jorgen]] (lure, turned revenant)\n    29→  - [[Golden Reach]] demons ([[Mandu|Markus]]/Danelle, incubus/succubus in Argos)\n    30→  - Hag Coven (Margaret, Gertrude, Tabitha — brew Pestilence from children's souls)\n    31→  - [[Mandu]] (corrupt Argos guard captain)\n    32→  - Reva (Machu Royal Advisor)\n    33→  - Tatiana (the Imposter of Machu)\n    34→  - [[Jafar]] (Sally's uncle, hired the Dons, placed [[Nathan Yanagan]] as spy)\n    35→  - [[Costa Una]] (traitor within STB)\n    36→  - Salina (Death Knight Captain), Lord Soth (Death Knight of Vodram)\n    37→- **Secondary Agent:** [[Ned'Zailok]] (Lich, Keeper of Death's Anchor in [[V-Mardov]])\n    38→  - Betrayed Casaval Dekun (Eladrin warrior)\n    39→- **Boss:** Forest Guardian / Forest Titan — awakened by [[Mordred]] using the [[Heart of the Wilde]]. Defeating it transports party to [[V-Mardov]].\n    40→\n    41→### Zenith of War (Mountain's Zenith — Vag'Mahar / Underdark)\n    42→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Forge]] (Garnet)\n    43→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of War]] (hammer-axe-javelin)\n    44→- **Eye:** Red Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    45→- **Primary Agent:** [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] (Illithilich — THE Central Villain)\n    46→  - Created the [[Spell Plague]] (with [[Alhoon Ren]]'s research)\n    47→  - Captured the [[Zodiac|Stag Zodiac]] to locate [[Mountain's Zenith]]\n    48→  - Holds [[Thorkon Fireforge]] captive; plans Ceremorphosis\n    49→  - Beholder relay network for psychic communication\n    50→  - Homunculus Thraalm (killed Ch. 66)\n    51→  - **Research Arm:** [[Alhoon Ren]] (double agent, secretly taking notes) + [[Alhoon Pollivar]] (Umber Hulk facility)\n    52→- **Allied Agent:** [[Oblivion Wayne]] (Vampire Queen of the Drow)\n    53→  - [[Ebon Wayne]] (husband, discovered [[Spell Plague]], captured Bat Zodiac)\n    54→  - Vampire-converted Drow army\n    55→  - Plans overland invasion\n    56→- **Allied Agent:** [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] (Puppetmaster of [[Vag'Mahar]])\n    57→  - Convinced [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] to betray [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    58→  - Then betrayed the deal; hunted all [[Forgesworn]]\n    59→  - Missing by late campaign\n    60→- **Nested Conspiracy:** The Dwarven Civil War\n    61→  - Three Thanes: Blackbeard, Brownbeard, Redbeard\n    62→  - Three secret police: Blackguard, Truth's Light, Silent Flame\n    63→  - **ALL THREE secretly run by A SINGLE MIND FLAYER**\n    64→  - Goal: use [[Anchor of War]] to breed better Duergar\n    65→\n    66→### Zenith of Pestilence (Winter's Zenith — Bynar)\n    67→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Faith]] (Sapphire)\n    68→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Pestilence]] (pan flute / Horn of Winter)\n    69→- **Eye:** Blue Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    70→- **Primary Agent:** [[Rimefang]] (Undead Dragon)\n    71→  - Raised by [[Vecna]], given a white dragon body via [[Black Hand Clan]] rebirth ritual\n    72→  - Ordered to \"open the [[Winter's Zenith]] and finish the Spellplague\"\n    73→  - Receives pestilence supply shipments\n    74→  - Made one last ride as sacrifice (Ch. 113)\n    75→- **Allied Agent:** Yuan-Ti / [[Scales of Falak]]\n    76→  - Stole the [[Anchor of Pestilence]] from the [[Sunless Citadel]]\n    77→  - Ruined the [[Winter's Curators]] (dragon-kin tribe)\n    78→  - Leaders: Umangi Shiva, Casstantinople Pserephonie, Lady Sessura Vaught, Mathia Nuum, Bree Jasnar\n    79→- **Guardian:** [[Frostragan]] (White Dragon, stole [[Heart of the Faith]])\n    80→- **Tiamat Connection:** [[Oblivion Wayne]] gave her soul to [[Tiamat]]. [[Tiamat]]'s avatar awakened late campaign."}]},"uuid":"4e3658b2-774b-4664-915f-233934242af1","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:23.785Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"67632c70-c10d-46b1-b7be-d5b65006a871","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"83736258-a6ef-466f-a8c3-c397d1f62313","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01TvdtpLmLh88UW4hRgkXVCA","toolUseID":"toolu_01TvdtpLmLh88UW4hRgkXVCA","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:28.739Z","uuid":"9d72ddad-484e-41ea-ad02-ad6a443e00d5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"a7e541f4-bed5-4030-ba62-368027d9c8ac","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TvdtpLmLh88UW4hRgkXVCA","type":"tool_result","content":"   150→### Sprocket & Bergs -- \"The Buddy Cop Duo\"\n   151→\n   152→- **How they met**: Original Brigade intake. Bergs was secretly planted by the Artificer's Guild; Sprocket is a divination wizard with Guild connections.\n   153→- **Core dynamic**: Comic relief and genuine camaraderie. Bergs's Summary 36 explicitly calls them \"buddy cop adventures.\" Bergs is the muscle, Sprocket is the brain. Bergs repeatedly rescues Sprocket from his own recklessness (caltrops -- Ch. 25, being swallowed by a frog monster -- Ch. 27).\n   154→- **Key bonding moment**: Bergs's Summary 36: \"Not looking too good is Sprocket, who got fwoooph'd by a spider. Someone ought to heal that guy so I don't have to find someone else to go on buddy cop adventures with.\"\n   155→- **Key conflict moment**: Bergs's Sporepocalypse theory (Ch. 36) -- Bergs becomes convinced that mushroom people will end the world and starts destroying them. The party thinks he's insane. Sprocket sides with reason over Bergs's paranoia, but without breaking their friendship.\n   156→- **Combat synergy**: Bergs throws Sprocket across a ravine (with a rope) in Ch. 24. Sprocket returns the favor by dropping fireballs to cover Bergs's retreats.\n   157→\n   158→---\n   159→\n   160→### Irethia & The Party -- \"The Secret-Keeper\"\n   161→\n   162→- **How they met**: Irethia appears around Ch. 16, already embedded with mysteries.\n   163→- **Core dynamic**: Irethia is the party's enigma. She has \"a draconic passenger\" (sorcerous bloodline), looks suspiciously like a wanted fugitive (similar to Ashara Everstar), and hears a voice saying \"Beware the Forest.\" Her Summary 20 is written entirely from internal monologue: \"Why must the gods shove me upon this journey, with such compromised strangers?\"\n   164→- **Key relationships**:\n   165→  - **Irethia & Brigit/Nyx/Varis**: The Vodram mission squad. They fight the Eye of Vecna together. Varis writes of all three with deep respect. This sub-group develops its own tight bond through shared extreme experiences in another plane.\n   166→  - **Irethia & Nocturnus**: In Summary 21, Randall notes: \"Nocturnus spots two hooded figures... He notices that 'Sally' looks remarkably like Irethia.\" This resemblance to Ashara Everstar creates suspicion that Irethia never fully dispels.\n   167→  - **Irethia & Tezka/Nyx**: Prophetically linked (Harvest Zenith room). Irethia wants to understand her powers; Nyx wants to save her sister; Tezka wants justice. Three quests converging on Dawnpass.\n   168→- **Key conflict**: Irethia's secrecy. She doesn't tell the party about the \"Beware the Forest\" warning. She holds back information. She's caught between trusting and fearing these \"strangers.\"\n   169→- **Where it stands**: By mid-campaign, trust is earned through combat. But Irethia remains the character most likely to have hidden motivations.\n   170→\n   171→---\n   172→\n   173→### Randal & The Party -- \"The Diplomatic Bard\"\n   174→\n   175→- **How they met**: Randal and Tezka arrive together in Ch. 10, delivered by cloaked figures with a note and payment.\n   176→- **Core dynamic**: Randal is the party's smoothest operator. He sings, he charms, he dispels, he inspires. Where Stalwart leads through force and Brigit through knowledge, Randal leads through charisma.\n   177→- **Key relationships**:\n   178→  - **Randal & Daina/Tiberius**: The pocket-dimension trio (Ch. 32-33). Randal's account reveals his dry humor and genuine affection for his companions. On Daina: \"Daina offered to scout ahead by running in a straight line for the enemy's flag. As good scouts do, she found several traps, and going above and beyond set them off to mark their locations.\"\n   179→  - **Randal & Nyx**: He passes Grimnak's warning to \"each of the party members without alerting 'Sally'\" (Ch. 21) -- demonstrates his role as the party's intelligence officer.\n   180→  - **Randal & Brigit**: In combat (Ch. 30), \"Randall plays a counter-charm for the late-comers... Inspiring Dana with a grunt and a nod.\" Brigit wakes from a mind-flayer stun after hearing \"the epic bard song.\"\n   181→- **Character Motivation doc**: \"There's something to be said for the power of ignorance... TRUTH beyond violence. Your search continues.... every day is another that slips away from you. Tick Tock. Death is closing in.\"\n   182→- **Where it stands**: Randal is the party member most aware that time is running out -- for him personally (he is dying) and for the world. This gives him urgency that manifests as gentle wisdom rather than panic.\n   183→\n   184→---\n   185→\n   186→### Tiberius & Stalwart -- \"The Dragonborn Brotherhood\"\n   187→\n   188→- **How they met**: Tiberius introduces himself in Ch. 8 when the party arrives in Argos.\n   189→- **Core dynamic**: Both dragonborn, both devout (Tiberius to Bahamut, Stalwart to Bahamut as well), both loud. They share a racial bond and a religious one. But Tiberius is the comic relief version of Stalwart's righteous fury.\n   190→- **Key bonding moment**: Tiberius's Summary 24. His entire account is written as a prayer to Bahamut, and he consistently refers to \"the dragonborn\" (Stalwart) with respect bordering on awe. They share the faith.\n   191→- **Key conflict moment**: \"Tiberius is now the shiniest blue color. I think it looks great, but he's a little sensitive about it. Apparently Dragons are very racist.\" (Summary 32) -- even within their bond, draconic prejudice creates friction.\n   192→- **Running gag**: Tiberius screams at buildings, scares orphans, and says \"Bahamut\" at inappropriate moments. Brigit consistently misspells his name in her notes.\n   193→\n   194→---\n   195→\n   196→### Nocturnus & The Nightmare Sword -- \"The Inner Demon\"\n   197→\n   198→This is not a character-to-character relationship but it is one of the campaign's most important dynamics. Nocturnus acquires the sentient evil sword Nightmare and it progressively corrupts him.\n   199→\n   200→- **Party response**: The entire party mobilizes against Nocturnus's corruption. Brigit tries to polymorph him into a bunny. Sylvia stuns him. The mushroom Sovereign gives him a pacifying spore. Sprocket \"listens to the thoughts of Nightmare and finds nothing good.\" Brigit uses Greater Restoration on him daily.\n   201→- **What this reveals**: The party loves Nocturnus enough to fight for his soul rather than abandon or kill him. This is the clearest expression of group loyalty in the campaign.\n   202→- **Nocturnus's own view** (Summary 23): He sees himself as the party's protector despite being their greatest internal threat. \"It becomes evident that I'll have to save these fools. Again.\" His self-narrative as savior makes his corruption tragic.\n   203→\n   204→---\n   205→\n   206→### Additional Key Pairings (Brief)\n   207→\n   208→| Pair | Dynamic | Defining Moment |\n   209→|------|---------|-----------------|\n   210→| Daina & Nocturnus | Combat partners, mutual respect | Daina grabs Nocturnus, screams \"slow fall!\" and rides him down a cliff (Ch. 29) |\n   211→| Stalwart & Linda (the chicken) | Paladin and his emotional support animal | Stalwart has a chicken named Linda that he carries everywhere. The Magister's Circle guards tell him to leave her behind. He refuses. |\n   212→| Nyx & her owl (Hedwig) | Warlock and familiar, genuine emotional bond | Nyx's owl is murdered by a drow warlock (Ch. 8). Summary 8: \"Nyx's beloved owl then died a quick and painless death, shrouded in glory and triumph.\" Deeply sarcastic, deeply sad. |\n   213→| Sprocket & Trixie | Rescue/romantic thread | Sprocket charges into a hobgoblin-infested tomb specifically because Trixie (pink-haired gnome) is captured. \"Sprocket immediately picked up on the description of Trixie and charged off to the rescue!\" |\n   214→| Bergs & The Lava | One-man running bit | Bergs stares into lava, eats pestilence, drinks gorgon blood, and generally puts things in his mouth that should kill him. The party is simultaneously horrified and impressed. |\n   215→| Daina & The Soul Bag | Moral weight | Daina carries the soul bag containing children's souls. She uses it pragmatically but it haunts her. She pulls out \"a child's soul as a solid cylindrical object\" (Ch. 19). |\n   216→\n   217→---\n   218→\n   219→<a name=\"part2\"></a>\n   220→\n   221→## Part 2: Character Arcs Through Relationships\n   222→\n   223→### Brigit Wildfire -- \"The Heart\"\n   224→\n   225→**Trust at start**: No one. Brigit is an orphaned druid from the wild outside Windhaven. Her parents were killed by fearful villagers. She trusts animals more than people.\n   226→\n   227→**Trust at end**: Everyone. Brigit's 109 chapter notes chronicle her transformation from suspicious loner to the party's emotional center. She takes notes for the group, plans strategies, heals the wounded, and polymorphs her allies to keep them alive. Her Summary 29 sign-off crystallizes this: \"I've got to be a safety net for these guys.\"\n   228→\n   229→**Primary driver**: The Stag / protecting nature. But increasingly, protecting her found family becomes inseparable from protecting nature itself.\n   230→\n   231→**Group role**: THE HEART. Brigit is the campaign's narrator, chronicler, strategist, healer, and moral compass. She is the character through whom the audience experiences the story (109/176 documents are her perspective). She worries about everyone, records everything, and fights like a demon in wolf form while thinking like a scholar in human form.\n   232→\n   233→**Key transformation through relationships**: Brigit enters the campaign wanting to be accepted. Through 136 chapters of shared trauma, she becomes the one who accepts others -- including Nocturnus (who she tries to save from Nightmare rather than condemn), Irethia (whose secrets she tolerates), and Dallor (whose cowardice she mocks but never makes lethal).\n   234→\n   235→---\n   236→\n   237→### Daina (Diesa) Fireforge -- \"The Forge\"\n   238→\n   239→**Trust at start**: No one. Daina is a wilderness loner whose father was murdered. She trusts her axe.\n   240→\n   241→**Trust at end**: The Fireforges (Bergs, eventually), Brigit, Stalwart. She remains combative and impulsive but channels her rage productively.\n   242→\n   243→**Primary driver**: Vengeance for her father / discovering her Fireforge heritage. Every dwarven revelation pulls her deeper into a destiny she didn't choose.\n   244→\n   245→**Group role**: THE FORCE. Daina hits things. She hits Dallor (repeatedly). She hits T-Rexes. She hits mind-flayers. She hits her allies when mind-controlled. She is the campaign's id -- pure action, raw emotion, unfiltered violence. But her rage is grief, and her violence is love expressed through protection.\n   246→\n   247→**Key transformation**: Daina enters as a vengeance-seeker hunting \"the black-eyed red-bearded stranger.\" Through the Fireforge revelations (Dvinter Castle, Magna Har, the phoenix), she discovers she is not just hunting a killer -- she is the heir to a royal weaponsmithing dynasty. Her \"rebirth\" (platinum hair, glowing armor) is the physical manifestation of a woman who has found her purpose beyond revenge.\n   248→\n   249→---\n   250→\n   251→### Stalwart Little Goldentooth -- \"The Shield\"\n   252→\n   253→**Trust at start**: His brothers (especially Stewart, deceased), Gran and the Little Orphanage, Bahamut.\n   254→\n   255→**Trust at end**: The party (grudgingly), Bahamut (devoutly), Linda (the chicken, unconditionally).\n   256→\n   257→**Primary driver**: Avenging Stewart's death at the Sunless Citadel / protecting innocents / pursuing the goblins who killed his brother.\n   258→\n   259→**Group role**: THE ANCHOR. Stalwart declares himself leader, nobody contests it (because he's an enormous golden dragonborn paladin who will argue until everyone gives up). He leads through stubbornness, divine sense, and physical presence. He also tortures prisoners and carves messages into living flesh, creating a moral tension the party navigates rather than resolves.\n   260→\n   261→**Key transformation**: Stalwart enters as a sailor-orphan with a vendetta against goblins. Through the prophecies, he learns he is linked to the Pestilence Zenith (Bynar). His personal quest expands from \"find the goblins\" to \"prevent the apocalypse.\" The hobgoblin prisoner scene (Ch. 24) -- where he mutilates a captive while Sprocket charges off to save Trixie and Bergs \"seeing one friend go full hero while the other went full villain\" -- defines his duality.\n   262→\n   263→---\n   264→\n   265→### Nyx Khamestria -- \"The Shadow\"\n   266→\n   267→**Trust at start**: Hedwig (her owl familiar). No one else.\n   268→\n   269→**Trust at end**: Brigit, Randal, Irethia, Tezka. She has learned to trust but never fully lets her guard down.\n   270→\n   271→**Primary driver**: Saving her sister Xyn from a demonic pact. Everything else is secondary.\n   272→\n   273→**Group role**: THE WILDCARD. Nyx does unexpected things -- lights braziers (Ch. 3), flies on brooms during combat (Ch. 28), convinces enemies to throw their swords away mid-fight. She is the party's chaos engine, but her chaos is intelligent. She solves problems through lateral thinking that looks reckless but is often brilliant.\n   274→\n   275→**Key transformation**: Nyx enters as a paranoid tiefling warlock running from her past. Her Summary 46 reveals a woman who has pieced together the cosmic puzzle of her patrons (Gabriel, Juliannia, Reynardine, Graz'zt). She has gone from \"who can I trust?\" to \"I have chosen to trust Reynardine, and I hope you can forgive me.\" This leap of faith -- trusting a manipulative archfey because she believes in the good person he once was -- is the campaign's most psychologically complex character moment.\n   276→\n   277→---\n   278→\n   279→### Sprocket Overspark -- \"The Clock\"\n   280→\n   281→**Trust at start**: The Alabaster Seat (who marked him for death and freed him simultaneously). His divination magic. Trixie (implied romantic interest).\n   282→\n   283→**Trust at end**: Brigit (his protector), the party (his reason to keep going despite knowing he will die).\n   284→\n   285→**Primary driver**: Fate itself. As a divination wizard marked for death, Sprocket knows his end is coming. His Character Motivation says: \"once you latch onto a possibility, really understand that one moment in time, then a true master of time itself can KNOW.\"\n   286→\n   287→**Group role**: THE BRAIN. Sprocket identifies threats, counterspells attacks, drops fireballs at critical moments, and uses mage hand for everything from retrieving torches to holding phylacteries to the ceiling. He is also the party's most fatalistic member -- he leans into danger because he has accepted his death.\n   288→\n   289→**Key transformation**: Sprocket carries \"a shard of a lich's soul in his pocket\" and hears Sabine's voice. He begins as a clever gnome wizard and becomes a vessel for cosmic forces. His recklessness (charging into caltrops, exploring alone) is not stupidity -- it is the behavior of someone who knows exactly when and how they will die and is racing to accomplish everything before the clock runs out.\n   290→\n   291→---\n   292→\n   293→### Nocturnus Wayne -- \"The Exile\"\n   294→\n   295→**Trust at start**: Himself. His mission to kill Aunt Oblivion Wayne.\n   296→\n   297→**Trust at end**: Complicated. He trusts the party to save him from the Nightmare sword, but he trusts himself to save the party from everything else. His Summary 23 worldview never fully evolves: \"I'll have to save these fools. Again.\"\n   298→\n   299→**Primary driver**: Vengeance against his aunt. After acquiring Nightmare and defeating his cousin Duskin (Ch. 36), his path leads to the Mountain Zenith and the First Forge.\n   300→\n   301→**Group role**: THE LONER. Nocturnus is the campaign's Batman -- operating alone, showing up at critical moments, delivering devastating attacks, and immediately retreating to brood. He needs only 4 hours of sleep. He takes first watch. He volunteers for solo missions.\n   302→\n   303→**Key transformation**: The Nightmare sword corruption is Nocturnus's central arc. He enters as a controlled, dangerous fighter and is consumed by a sentient evil weapon. The party's response -- fighting to save him rather than fighting him -- forces Nocturnus to confront that he is not alone, no matter how much he pretends to be. The pacifying spore covering half his face is a visible metaphor: Nocturnus is literally half himself, half something darker.\n   304→\n   305→---\n   306→\n   307→<a name=\"part3\"></a>\n   308→\n   309→## Part 3: Production-Critical Dynamics for the Pilot\n   310→\n   311→The pilot covers the party's first encounter (Chapters 0-2): recruitment into the Scarlet Tunic Brigade, the sewer descent, the betrayal by Jorgen/Finkle, the ritual chamber, and the sealed Raven Queen door.\n   312→\n   313→### Relationships Established in the Pilot Scope\n   314→\n   315→**1. The Strangers' Awkwardness (All PCs)**\n   316→- These characters do NOT know each other at the start. They are strangers forced together by a military summons. Panel composition should reflect this:\n   317→  - **Physical distance**: Characters stand apart, not together. Personal space buffers between everyone.\n   318→  - **Eye contact**: Avoided or suspicious. Side-glances, wary assessments.\n   319→  - **Body language**: Closed postures. Arms crossed, shoulders turned slightly away, weight on back foot (ready to leave).\n   320→\n   321→**2. Sprocket -- The Gnome Alone**\n   322→- Sprocket arrives in a carriage, studying fate. He is introduced APART from the group.\n   323→- When joining the others, he should be physically lower (gnome height) and slightly ahead or behind -- never centered in the group.\n   324→- **Visual cue**: He is always looking at something the others can't see (fate, divination, portents).\n   325→\n   326→**3. Brigit -- Wolf to Woman**\n   327→- Brigit arrives at the town walls in wolf form and shifts to human. This establishes her duality immediately.\n   328→- **Visual cue**: When in human form, her posture should retain something feral -- weight low, eyes scanning the perimeter, standing at the edge of the group rather than its center.\n   329→- She should be positioned near exits and open spaces, uncomfortable with walls.\n   330→\n   331→**4. Stalwart -- Commands the Room**\n   332→- Stalwart is a golden-scaled dragonborn who \"commands attention on every street.\" He should DOMINATE panel composition through sheer size and presence.\n   333→- **Visual cue**: He stands tallest, takes up the most space, and faces forward while others angle away. He is the only one who looks like he belongs in a military context.\n   334→- Linda (the chicken) is not present in the pilot, but any companion animal (or lack thereof) should be noted.\n   335→\n   336→**5. Nyx -- The Hidden One**\n   337→- Nyx is introduced \"scribbling secrets in a candlelit inn room.\" She is INTERIOR while the others are exterior.\n   338→- **Visual cue**: She should be in shadows, partially obscured, face lit from below by candle or magic. Her horns should be hinted at but not fully visible until her full reveal.\n   339→- When joining the group, she should stand furthest from Stalwart (the paladin -- natural warlock/paladin tension) and closest to Dallor (both outsiders).\n   340→\n   341→**6. Daina -- The Small Fury**\n   342→- Quick-cut introduction: \"a furiously short man elbowing through crowds\" (the pilot bible misgenders her or describes another character -- Daina is female). Daina should be shown as physically small but OCCUPYING space aggressively.\n   343→- **Visual cue**: She pushes through crowds rather than navigating them. She is in motion while others are still. Her axe should be visible at all times.\n   344→\n   345→**7. The Finkle Dynamic**\n   346→- Finkle Hollingsworth is the party's first shared antagonist: a three-foot gnome bureaucrat who commands through institutional authority. All PCs should be unified in their discomfort with him, even before they trust each other.\n   347→- **Panel composition**: Finkle should be at the TOP of his triangle diagram (literally above them despite being the shortest figure in the room). The PCs should be shown exchanging skeptical looks -- the first moment of shared feeling.\n   348→\n   349→**8. The Jorgen Betrayal -- The Moment of Bonding**"}]},"uuid":"242d15aa-4683-4678-8d63-debbdf91e232","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:28.739Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a7e541f4-bed5-4030-ba62-368027d9c8ac","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

She married [[Ebon Wayne]] and used the [[Bat Zodiac]] to create alpha vampires, building an army of turned drow with innate loyalty. She is obsessed with the ancient Warforged army, believing warforged are key to dominating the Illithid. She is Nocturnus's prophesied target.\n    19→\n    20→## Role in the Campaign\n    21→\n    22→Oblivion serves Vecna's agenda in the Underdark, specifically targeting the War's Zenith. Her vampire army and warforged obsession make her one of the campaign's most dangerous antagonists. {"parentUuid":"9ae51254-a3b8-4e03-91f4-3aa556af6873","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NAzkuDpLwEjdZiNkeVBWYd","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: npc-major\n     4→name: \"Mordred\"\n     5→aliases: [\"V-Mordred\", \"Finkle (disguise)\"]\n     6→race: \"Unknown (divine agent)\"\n     7→class: \"Necromancer / Vecna's Champion\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Vecna Sworn]]\"\n     9→location: \"Various\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0 (disguised as Finkle)\"\n    12→source: [\"NPC Catalog\", \"Player Notes\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/npc-art-source/Vecna Sworn/Machu/Mordred\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Mordred is Vecna's primary mortal agent and the campaign's first villain. He infiltrated the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] recruitment disguised as **Finkle Hollingsworth**, a three-foot gnome bureaucrat, orchestrating the pilot's ritual chamber betrayal. His true form is depicted in multiple versions (\"Mordred Completed\", \"New Mordred\"), suggesting ongoing transformation.\n    19→\n    20→> **CRITICAL CORRECTION**: Finkle Hollingsworth is a **Gnome** (NOT a Goblin). He is the disguise Mordred used to infiltrate the Scarlet Tunic Brigade.\n    21→\n    22→## Role in the Campaign\n    23→\n    24→Mordred orchestrated the events of Chapters 0-2: the recruitment, the kobold attacks, the sewer descent, and the ritual chamber. He drained every kobold in the room to create ghouls, revealed three enslaved adventurers, and nearly completed the necromantic ritual before the party shattered the phylactery and freed [[Sabine Ra]].\n    25→\n    26→His plan continues through the [[Vecna Sworn]] organization, with agents at each Zenith working toward Vecna's ascension.\n    27→\n    28→## Faction\n    29→\n    30→[[Vecna Sworn]] -- Machu & Death Zenith division.\n    31→"}]},"uuid":"f54c1e6f-feb9-4f92-91ad-21ffe8b07f95","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:31.498Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9ae51254-a3b8-4e03-91f4-3aa556af6873","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"f1ff2016-9b2d-43a1-92fe-d39d72a65d5c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013CFfCV8sosRaEsKA2548Tg","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Fey Courts\"\n     4→aliases: [\"The Four Courts\", \"Courts of the Feywild\"]\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"Various Fey Lords\"\n     7→headquarters: \"[[Feywild]]\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"world-bible.md\", \"npcs-catalog.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# The Fey Courts\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→The [[Feywild|Fae Wildes]] are organized into four seasonal courts, each with its own domain and leadership. The courts are deeply entangled with the campaign's politics through [[Reynaldine]]'s machinations and the Convergence event.\n    17→\n    18→## The Four Courts\n    19→\n    20→### Spring Court — Brier Patch\n    21→- **Fey Lord:** Evylanndrin of the Spring Court\n    22→- **Eladrin Representative:** [[Haden Dar]] (Platinum Seat of Illusionist Covenant)\n    23→\n    24→### Summer Court — Flowerfield\n    25→- **Fey Lord:** Juliannia of the Summer Court\n    26→- **Eladrin Representative:** Jing Lin (Voice of Potential)\n    27→\n    28→### Autumn Court — Reaper's Bounty\n    29→- **Fey Lord:** [[Reynaldine]] of the Autumn Court\n    30→- **Role in Campaign:** The most politically active court. [[Reynaldine]] serves [[Vecna]] while pursuing his own agenda — to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel (archangel of kindness).\n    31→- **Key Actions:**\n    32→  - Hired Annie Hallowbrook to deliver [[Kelik Waystar]] to [[WindHaven]]\n    33→  - Used [[Xyn]] (Nyx's twin sister) as messenger\n    34→  - Threatened to disrupt pestilence shipments to [[Rimefang]] as leverage\n    35→  - Invoked the Ritual of the Stone Table through Umbara Skuttle\n    36→  - True plan revealed Ch. 133 — orchestrated everything for his own ascension\n    37→  - \"Crippled\" Ch. 106; cloak fell revealing leeches forming a portal\n    38→- **Queen of Thorns** — Referenced Autumn Court figure\n    39→\n    40→### Winter Court — Snowcrest Sanctum\n    41→- **Known Members:**\n    42→  - Grumblesin of the Winter Court\n    43→  - Silence (Timothy of the Feywild) — Therzadun's vessel\n    44→  - Tallow (Squire of Winter)\n    45→  - Yeth Hounds\n    46→- **Eladrin Representative:** Tumnus Driftwalker (Nic of the [[Winter's Curators]])\n    47→\n    48→> **CRITICAL NOTE:** [[Reynaldine]] (Fey Lord of the Autumn Court) is a DISTINCT entity from [[Reynardine]] (Nyx's patron, \"the Madness Bringer\"). Similar names, different characters.\n    49→\n    50→## The Convergence (Late Campaign)\n    51→\n    52→All planes began converging into the Feywild:\n    53→- Orchestrated by [[Reynaldine]]\n    54→- **Stone Table Ritual:** sacrifice + benefactor = sovereignty\n    55→- Knights needed: [[Riven Fen|Fen]], [[Josh Coolbreeze|Josh]], [[Varis Liadon|Varis]], [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]\n    56→- [[Graz'zt]] swapped with Gabriel at the ritual\n    57→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] forced both himself and [[Graz'zt]] onto the Stone Table\n    58→- **Result:** [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild\n    59→- Shattering of Stone Table shatters the Feywild and Mardov\n    60→"}]},"uuid":"2310b618-9f51-4e39-8b6e-4130e9574a86","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:36.005Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f1ff2016-9b2d-43a1-92fe-d39d72a65d5c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

Each of the 12 Maghar specializes in creating a different type of fireforged item. Their craft carries the heaviest cost in all of [[Mardov]]:\n    17→\n    18→> **A bit of yerself is imparted in each of yer creations.** There is nothing worth more than yer soul.\n    19→> — [[Thorkon Fireforge]]\n    20→\n    21→The [[Forgesworn]] were hunted to near-extinction by [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] after [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s betrayal was exposed.\n    22→\n    23→## The 12 Maghar\n    24→\n    25→| Title | Specialty | Known Holder | Status |\n    26→|-------|-----------|-------------|--------|\n    27→| Maghar of the Axe | Axes | [[Thorkon Fireforge]] | Captured by [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] |\n    28→| Maghar of the Hammer | Hammers | [[Hlin Blunderbeat]] | Deceased (childbirth) |\n    29→| Maghar of the Breastplate | Armor | [[Bartrim Hadrin]] | Active (exiled, leads Bartrim's Brezerkers) |\n    30→| Maghar of the Gear | Mechanisms | [[Decan Smithrunner]] | Active (runs the Dragon's Carapace) |\n    31→| Maghar of the Sword | Swords | Evelyn \"SteelHardt\" Hadrin | Deceased (killed by [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]) |\n    32→| Maghar of the Belt | Belts | [[Reginald Providence]] | Deceased (Therzadun Sworn, killed Ch. 104-105) |\n    33→| (6 others) | Unknown | Unknown | Unknown |\n    34→\n    35→## Goals & Motivation\n    36→\n    37→- **Survival** — avoid extinction at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s hands\n    38→- **Freedom** — end the forced creation of weapons for kings\n    39→- **The Warforged Secret** — prevent the creation of more Warforged, which consume dwarven souls\n    40→\n    41→## Members & Allies\n    42→\n    43→### The Guilded Rite Rebellion\n    44→A rebellion protecting surviving [[Forgesworn]]:\n    45→- [[Decan Smithrunner]] — Rebellion leader, [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s trusted contact\n    46→- [[Dorrin Hammer]] — Bastard son of Rowena Bronzebeard, the only one who can build/control Warforged\n    47→\n    48→### Bartrim's Brezerkers (Mercenary Company)\n    49→- [[Bartrim Hadrin]] — Exiled Maghar, \"The Mole,\" member of the disbanded Platinum Band\n    50→- Dierdra Crestfallen — Cleric of [[Bahamut]]\n    51→- Dune — Earth Genasi Prince Seeker\n    52→- Sylvia Hadrin — Bartrim's daughter, takes up her father's sword\n    53→- Yousef Redvine — Arcaneum Collector\n    54→\n    55→### Heirs\n    56→- [[Daina Fireforge]] — Daughter of [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], heiress to the [[First Forge]]\n    57→- [[Bergs Fireforge]] — Twin soul with [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]], also connected to the Fireforge lineage\n    58→\n    59→## Enemies\n
