They judge the world by measuring deeds through two philosophical lenses: Justice and Righteousness.\n    20→\n    21→### Members\n    22→| Name | Role |\n    23→|------|------|\n    24→| Balazar Keerin | Platinum Dragonborn, Archbishop of [[Bahamut]] |\n    25→| Kali Loxheart | Paladin of [[Bahamut]] |\n    26→| Raelyn Nabetta | Priestess, Emissary to [[Bynar]] |\n    27→| Verin Farmstart | New Cleric of [[Bahamut]] |\n    28→\n    29→---\n    30→\n    31→## Children of the Forest\n    32→\n    33→Ancient group connected to the Progenitors.\n    34→\n    35→### Original Members\n    36→- Bugz, Cycle (lost to [[Xerxes]]), Frost, Haystack\n    37→\n    38→### Reborn Members\n    39→- Bark, Blossom, Breeze, Chlora, Lumin, Scale (Yuan-Ti possessed), Sylva, Sprig\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→## Children of the Sun-Father, Pelor\n    44→- Rene Aigner (Cleric)\n    45→- Westron Hillbrent (Paladin)\n    46→\n    47→---\n    48→\n    49→## Fatesworn of the Raven Queen\n    50→- Goodwin Ridell (Cleric)\n    51→- Lainy Yarrow (Gloomstalker Cleric)\n    52→- Regina Crowe (Priestess)\n    53→\n    54→---\n    55→\n    56→## Grovewardens of Avandra\n    57→- Balaran Verdanfyre & Meepa (Cleric)\n    58→- Idonia Halbren (Cleric)\n    59→\n    60→---"}]},"uuid":"509e273c-4689-4229-91f9-c2c8ca9561b5","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:37.370Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a23af249-a411-4360-808b-3172f8476034","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"fdbb48d2-7b17-44bf-9e24-edf1d171baec","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0178B3uidYmzMZDGSkNMYQTF","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Therzadun Sworn\"\n     4→aliases: []\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"Therzadun (sealed)\"\n     7→headquarters: \"Behind the Iron Doors at Mountain's Zenith\"\n     8→status: \"defeated\"\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Therzadun Sworn\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→Servants of Therzadun, the god of destruction. **Separate from [[Vecna]] but entangled** — their goals sometimes align, sometimes conflict. Therzadun is sealed behind Iron Doors at the [[Mountain's Zenith]].\n    17→\n    18→## Members\n    19→\n    20→- **[[Reginald Providence]]** — Lich of Therzadun, [[Forgesworn|Maghar of the Belt]]. In true form a Kenku, disguised with \"plastic surgery.\" Wore the cloak of the [[Raven Queen]]. Killed by [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] (Ch. 104-105), unleashing the Phoenix maul.\n    21→- **Grumble** — Reginald's apprentice and manservant\n    22→- **Vellem the Beholder** — Allied with Reginald against the party\n    23→- **Silence (Timothy)** — Therzadun's vessel, unmasked by moonbeam (Ch. 105)\n    24→- Refurbished Warforged\n    25→\n    26→## Goals & Motivation\n    27→\n    28→- Free Therzadun from behind the Iron Doors\n    29→- Use the [[Anchor of War]] for Therzadun's purposes (conflicting with [[Peraxis Phyrexia]]'s goals)\n    30→\n    31→## Key Events\n    32→\n    33→- **Ch. 104-105:** [[Reginald Providence]] killed. His true kenku form revealed. [[Anchor of War]] silenced. {"parentUuid":"7c28cf5a-79ef-4d30-875a-79b9211529e9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_013UHsqyLod7CG3fD2eG8e85","toolUseID":"toolu_013UHsqyLod7CG3fD2eG8e85","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:44.554Z","uuid":"0a335483-e90b-4f4b-b68e-3776f7c1d7f7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"04a08e93-00d6-4c65-b2ad-4d4209e476c7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013UHsqyLod7CG3fD2eG8e85","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Nocturnus Wayne\"\n     5→aliases: [\"The Loner\", \"The Exile\", \"Darkest Knight\"]\n     6→race: \"Drow\"\n     7→class: \"Monk / Vampire\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive (undead)\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Summary 23\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Nocturnus/Nocturnus Wayne -- Post Vampire.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Nocturnus Wayne is the party's dark knight -- a drow monk turned vampire who kills gods because someone has to, and because the darkness in him is already paid for. He operates alone by preference and in the dark by necessity. His quest to destroy his aunt [[Oblivion Wayne]], the drow vampire queen, expanded into a cosmic role as godslayer and shadow champion. He is the party member who scares everyone and makes everyone laugh, often simultaneously.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **tall, lean male** with **deathly pale grey-white skin** that reads as corpselike in any lighting. His **long white-platinum hair** falls past his shoulders in lank, straight strands, framing a face with **dark, sunken predatory eyes** set deep in shadowed sockets. His features are gaunt, angular, and aristocratic in the way that death is aristocratic.\n    23→\n    24→He wears **black spiked armor** covered in organic, thorn-like blade protrusions -- the armor looks less forged and more grown, as though darkness itself crystallized into protective plate. Dark leather and metal layers are secured with multiple **buckles and straps**, creating a silhouette of controlled menace. Behind his shoulders, **wing-like blade protrusions** extend upward -- not wings he can fly with, but decorative barbs of dark metal that echo a bat's wingspan.\n    25→\n    26→In his right hand he grips a **greatsword** -- his companion weapon, Bane -- with casual, intimate familiarity. His left hand drips with **fresh blood**, the only warm color in his entire palette. The blood catches light that his skin does not.\n    27→\n    28→The color palette is intentional: cool blue-grey, black armor, white hair, and the singular shock of red blood. Every element reads gothic, vampiric, and dangerous. He is the visual embodiment of the party's darkest capabilities.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality"}]},"uuid":"70be62d9-676c-4046-98da-fdda33414a4c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:44.554Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"04a08e93-00d6-4c65-b2ad-4d4209e476c7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"6b7857c4-f87a-44c2-b99d-6fdcb7dd6bee","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Mk6qCnf7xsCUehvM71cxb2","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Stalwart Little Goldentooth\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Stalwart\", \"Lit of the Summerborn\", \"The Anchor\", \"The Shield\"]\n     6→race: \"Gold Dragonborn\"\n     7→class: \"Paladin\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[Bahamut]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"DM Notes\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Stalwart/Stalwart Little.png\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Stalwart/Stalwart Little Goldentooth -- Lit of the Summerborn.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Stalwart Little Goldentooth is the self-appointed leader of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] -- a title nobody contests because he is an enormous golden dragonborn paladin who will argue until everyone gives up. He is the party's anchor: righteous, stubborn, divinely empowered, and capable of channeling divine magic without a deity through pure conviction. His personal quest to avenge his brother Stewart's death expanded into a cosmic role linked to the [[Pestilence Zenith]] at [[Bynar]].\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **gold-scaled dragonborn** of imposing stature, standing well above most humanoids. His scales are a warm, burnished gold that catch and reflect light, giving him a naturally regal presence. His head features a rounded snout with small facial horns, and his eyes are a piercing yellow-amber. His clawed gold feet and thick tail complete the draconic silhouette.\n    23→\n    24→He wears **gleaming silver plate armor** with ornate gold filigree -- swirling decorative patterns etched into the pauldrons, breastplate, greaves, and gauntlets. The armor is pristine and well-maintained (he polishes it obsessively). A **crimson cloth tabard or sash** is draped around his neck and across one shoulder, providing a bold warm accent against the silver steel. His belt is dark leather with a purple-toned buckle.\n    25→\n    26→In his right hand he wields a **longsword** with a gold crossguard and red inlaid detail, its blade long and polished. In his left he carries a large **kite shield** -- silver-grey steel decorated with elegant gold filigree scrollwork. The shield is as much a character as a weapon; it faces threats before Stalwart consciously does.\n    27→\n    28→His overall silhouette reads as classic paladin -- armored, shielded, upright, commanding. The gold scales against silver armor create a visual metaphor: he is both the treasure and the vault that protects it.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality"}]},"uuid":"033e09df-5772-4082-8ea2-d789387cb716","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:45.226Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6b7857c4-f87a-44c2-b99d-6fdcb7dd6bee","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"81c43797-a53d-4ba8-8c73-ee4d0e9ca1ac","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AGjqU1if4H8NZC42J2Whr7","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Artificer's Guild\"\n     4→aliases: [\"The Guild\"]\n     5→category: organization\n     6→leader: \"[[Xerxes]] (compromised)\"\n     7→headquarters: \"Argos (Chapter)\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Artificer's Guild\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→Founded by [[Sabine Ra]]'s avatar (the [[Raven Queen]]) and entrusted to her husband [[Xerxes]]. The [[Artificer's Guild]] specializes in animancy (the magic of life) — the counterpart to the Circle of the Murder's necromancy (death magic).\n    17→\n    18→[[Mordred]] gave up his phylactery specifically to infiltrate and take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body in the process.\n    19→\n    20→## Members — Argos Chapter\n    21→\n    22→| Name | Notes |\n    23→|------|-------|\n    24→| Andegold Woodsmirk | — |\n    25→| [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen Brighthallow]] | Dragonborn Ranger, cursed |\n    26→| Gold Cloud | — |\n    27→| Harkov | — |\n    28→| Kyrenic A | — |\n    29→| Ronald Drenson | — |\n    30→| Rubin Gage | — |\n    31→| Orik Gnar | Reborn Half-Orc of the [[Raven Queen]] |\n    32→\n    33→## Key NPCs\n    34→\n    35→- **[[Xerxes]]** — Founder, [[Sabine Ra]]'s husband. Compromised by [[Mordred]].\n    36→- **Walden** — Artificer's Son (created by the fractured [[Xerxes]])\n    37→- **[[Bergs Fireforge]]** — Planted within the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] as a Guild agent\n    38→\n    39→## Goals & Motivation\n    40→\n    41→- Oppose [[Mordred]] and [[Vecna]]'s necromantic operations\n    42→- Preserve the balance between life and death magic\n    43→- Protect the legacy of [[Sabine Ra]]\n    44→\n    45→## History\n    46→\n    47→- Founded by the [[Raven Queen]] (Sabine's avatar), entrusted to [[Xerxes]]\n    48→- [[Mordred]] infiltrated the Guild by sacrificing his phylactery\n    49→- [[Mordred]] corrupted the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]]\n    50→- The party rescues the Guild (Ch. 3-5)\n    51→"}]},"uuid":"aba34a2e-1f1d-4c93-86a6-2897e057fbed","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:46.798Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"81c43797-a53d-4ba8-8c73-ee4d0e9ca1ac","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"56aa4e81-da6b-4880-8e43-e94915f7515a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018U1Tk7nNHALkvEENTBctQa","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Scarlet Tunic Brigade\"\n     4→aliases: [\"STB\", \"The Brigade\"]\n     5→category: military\n     6→leader: \"[[Finkle Hollingsworth]] (front) / [[Mordred]] (true)\"\n     7→headquarters: \"Argos\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"chapters-act1-2.md\", \"Varys' backstory.txt\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→The Scarlet Tunic Brigade is a private military company that serves as the campaign's opening deception. On the surface, it is a legitimate mercenary organization that recruits adventurers, defends towns, and covers evacuations. In truth, it is a **soul-harvesting operation** run by the lich [[Mordred]] through his vessel [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] (a Gnome, NOT a Goblin).\n    17→\n    18→The Brigade has harvested approximately **2,000 adventurer souls** by luring recruits through [[Jorgen]] and leading them to ritual chambers beneath cities.\n    19→\n    20→## Goals & Motivation\n    21→\n    22→- **Public Face:** Recruit adventurers, defend frontier towns, maintain order\n    23→- **True Purpose:** Harvest souls for [[Mordred]]'s necromantic rituals\n    24→- **End Goal:** Gather enough soul energy to facilitate [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion\n    25→\n    26→## Members\n    27→\n    28→### Leadership\n    29→- [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] — COO (Gnome, possessed by [[Mordred]]). Bookish bureaucrat persona. Later freed and becomes a genuine ally.\n    30→- [[Mordred]] / [[Mordred|Ramses]] — The lich controlling the operation. [[Sabine Ra]]'s former teacher.\n    31→\n    32→### Known Agents\n    33→- [[Jorgen]] — Human ranger in a gold tabard. Lure who leads recruits to their deaths. Killed by [[Mordred]], becomes a revenant chanting \"KILL THE GNOME.\"\n    34→- Stromble and Grom — Half-ogre henchmen / bodyguards\n    35→- [[Costa Una]] — Traitor within the Brigade\n    36→- [[Dockson]] — An honorable paladin, high-ranking STB general (legitimate side)\n    37→\n    38→### Crime Branch Members\n    39→- Axel Hutch, Hugs Grey, Knifes Henn, Mace Grumm — Muscle\n    40→- Leah Ulstran — Royal Ambassador\n    41→- Rojas Drezel — Mage\n    42→- Slitz Gnard, Lugs — Members\n    43→- The Librarian — Member\n    44→- Drab (5 members) — Cover identities (Entryway, Kitchen, Waiter staff)\n    45→\n    46→## Allies & Enemies\n    47→\n    48→### Allies (True)\n    49→- [[Vecna]] — Ultimate patron\n    50→- [[Mordred]]'s network — [[Golden Reach]] demons, Hag Coven, [[Mandu]]\n    51→\n    52→### Allies (Apparent)\n    53→- Frontier towns who rely on STB for defense\n    54→- The party (before the reveal)\n    55→\n    56→### Enemies\n    57→- [[Artificer's Guild]]\n    58→- The party (after Ch. 2)\n    59→\n    60→## History"}]},"uuid":"447f7e0e-ebf6-4490-8e0b-fdae2abcb323","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:47.260Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"56aa4e81-da6b-4880-8e43-e94915f7515a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"cc0f485b-7c47-4953-947c-19686c3ea408","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SFmGvLKkrJj162ms7CSjLU","type":"tool_result","content":"   340→\n   341→**6. Daina -- The Small Fury**\n   342→- Quick-cut introduction: \"a furiously short man elbowing through crowds\" (the pilot bible misgenders her or describes another character -- Daina is female). Daina should be shown as physically small but OCCUPYING space aggressively.\n   343→- **Visual cue**: She pushes through crowds rather than navigating them. She is in motion while others are still. Her axe should be visible at all times.\n   344→\n   345→**7. The Finkle Dynamic**\n   346→- Finkle Hollingsworth is the party's first shared antagonist: a three-foot gnome bureaucrat who commands through institutional authority. All PCs should be unified in their discomfort with him, even before they trust each other.\n   347→- **Panel composition**: Finkle should be at the TOP of his triangle diagram (literally above them despite being the shortest figure in the room). The PCs should be shown exchanging skeptical looks -- the first moment of shared feeling.\n   348→\n   349→**8. The Jorgen Betrayal -- The Moment of Bonding**\n   350→- When Jorgen arrives bleeding with kobolds, the strangers must fight together for the first time. This is the EXACT moment the group dynamic begins.\n   351→- **Body language shift**: Characters move from individual fighting stances to covering each other's flanks. This should happen organically -- Brigit (wolf form) placing herself between danger and the door, Sprocket using mage hand from a distance, Stalwart stepping in front of the weaker party members.\n   352→- **Eye contact shift**: The first genuine eye contact should happen mid-combat. A glance between Brigit and Daina. A nod from Stalwart to Sprocket. These are involuntary -- survival instinct creates trust before friendship does.\n   353→\n   354→**9. The Underground Descent -- Physical Proximity Forced**\n   355→- The sewer descent and dungeon corridors force physical proximity. Characters who maintained distance on the surface are now shoulder-to-shoulder.\n   356→- **Visual cue**: Claustrophobic panel composition. Characters filling the frame. Torchlight creating a shared bubble of visibility that includes ALL of them, whether they want it or not.\n   357→- **Key composition rule**: Stalwart leads. Nocturnus is at the rear (his drow darkvision makes him the natural rearguard). Brigit and Daina are in the middle. Sprocket and Nyx are protected between the fighters.\n   358→\n   359→**10. The Ritual Chamber Reveal -- The Shared Awe**\n   360→- The cathedral-scale ritual chamber (four supernatural waterfalls, central altar) is the pilot's full-page reveal. For the first time, ALL characters should be looking at the SAME thing.\n   361→- **Visual cue**: All faces lit from below by the four-colored light. All mouths slightly open. All bodies still. This is the moment the audience sees them as a GROUP for the first time, unified by wonder rather than combat.\n   362→\n   363→---\n   364→\n   365→### Panel Composition Quick-Reference for Key Dynamics\n   366→\n   367→| Relationship | Visual Shorthand | Panel Proximity |\n   368→|--------------|-----------------|-----------------|\n   369→| Brigit & Daina | Brigit in wolf form flanking Daina, OR Brigit watching Daina charge forward | Adjacent, Brigit slightly behind |\n   370→| Stalwart & everyone | Stalwart tallest figure, always facing the threat | Center-front or leading position |\n   371→| Sprocket & Brigit | Sprocket small, Brigit bending down or looking back to check on him | Sprocket lower in frame |\n   372→| Nyx & shadows | Nyx partially in shadow, face lit from one side only | Edge of frame, often cut off |\n   373→| Daina & Dallor | Daina's fist near Dallor's face, or Dallor flinching | Close but antagonistic |\n   374→| Nocturnus & everyone | Separate from the group, watching from above or behind | Deliberately isolated in frame |\n   375→| The whole party | Fragmented in surface scenes, unified in underground scenes | Distance decreases as story progresses |\n   376→\n   377→---\n   378→\n   379→### Summary of Pilot Relationship Seeds\n   380→\n   381→The pilot should plant these seeds WITHOUT resolving them:\n   382→\n   383→1. **Brigit watches everyone** -- she is the observer. Show her eyes tracking other characters in crowd scenes.\n   384→2. **Daina doesn't need anyone** -- she fights alone until combat forces cooperation. Her first assisted kill should surprise her.\n   385→3. **Stalwart assumes command** -- nobody asked him to lead. Nobody stops him either. This ambiguity is important.\n   386→4. **Nyx keeps secrets** -- she is writing in her journal when others are socializing. She knows things she isn't sharing.\n   387→5. **Sprocket knows something bad is coming** -- his divination gives him a haunted quality. He is the only one NOT surprised by the betrayal.\n   388→6. **Nocturnus is absent until needed** -- he should appear from nowhere in combat, deliver devastating damage, then fade back.\n   389→7. **The group is NOT friends yet** -- they are professionals forced together. The underground descent creates proximity. The ritual chamber creates shared wonder. The betrayal creates shared survival instinct. Friendship comes later.\n   390→\n   391→---\n   392→\n   393→## Appendix: Zodiac/Zenith Groupings and Their Relationship Implications\n   394→\n   395→The prophetic groupings reveal which characters' fates are cosmically intertwined:\n   396→\n   397→| Zenith | Anchor | Characters | Zodiac Patron | Relationship Implication |\n   398→|--------|--------|-----------|--------------|------------------------|\n   399→| Forest (Macchu) | Death | Brigit, Varis, Sprocket | Elk, Doe, Rabbit | The naturalists -- connected by reverence for the wild |\n   400→| Mountain (Vagna Har) | War | Daina, Nocturnus, Bergs | Wolf, Wolverine, (Bat/Cthulu?) | The warriors -- connected by violence and family legacy |\n   401→| Winter (Bynar) | Pestilence | Randal, Stalwart, Dallor | Serpent, Turtle, Mongoose | The seekers -- connected by truth and duty |\n   402→| Harvest (Tygras Logar) | Famine | Nyx, Irethia, Tezka | Owl, Fox, Mouse | The tieflings/outsiders -- connected by persecution and hidden power |\n   403→\n   404→These groupings foreshadow the campaign's endgame: the party will eventually split into four quests across four continents. The relationships within each Zenith group must be stronger than those between groups, because these are the people who will face their respective apocalyptic anchors together.\n   405→\n   406→"}]},"uuid":"a0e93a95-9e3c-420d-9e7c-dac956cc6df7","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:49.442Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cc0f485b-7c47-4953-947c-19686c3ea408","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"9f957451-075f-4de5-9584-2dc1701bf2ea","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013rgzCYBCos46r8sz4ar69Q","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"timeline-act1-2\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/timeline-act1-2.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign — Timeline (Acts 1 & 2)\n     8→\n     9→> Chronological key events extracted from DM chapter notes, Chapters 0-37.\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Prehistory / Deep Lore\n    14→\n    15→- **Ancient era**: Mammothborn civilization exists; Tiamat eradicates them. Kolyma, the Last Curator, survives in the Rod of Sanctuary.\n    16→- **Ancient era**: The Raven Queen establishes the four Zeniths (Forest, Winter, Mountain, Harvest) with corresponding Anchors (Death, Pestilence, War, Famine) to maintain cosmic balance.\n    17→- **Ancient era**: 13 Dueregar fortresses built, each imprisoning a Phoenix as seals to lock away the First Forge beneath Vag'Mahar.\n    18→- **Ancient era**: The Sleepless (Aboleth), older than gods, takes residence in the Purelake deep beneath the surface.\n    19→- **Centuries ago**: Sabine Ra, avatar of the Raven Queen, founds two colleges of magic: the Circle of the Murder (necromancy/death) and the Artificer's Guild (animancy/life). She entrusts them to Ramses (her teacher) and Xerxes (her husband).\n    20→- **Centuries ago**: Ramses grows jealous; attempts to grant himself and Sabine eternal life through lichdom. Sabine rejects the ritual; Ramses forces it upon her. Sabine starves her lich form by refusing to consume souls, crystallizing into a Demi-Lich.\n    21→- **Centuries ago**: Ramses becomes Mordred, an ancient lich. Xerxes and the Artificer's Guild begin centuries-long war against him.\n    22→- **~500 years ago**: Xerxes achieves his unique form -- a colony of living organisms (moss, plants) that inherit his memories and soul.\n    23→- **Decades ago**: Tiberious D. Rex resurrects Bartrim Hadrin using divine magic of Bahamut, placing a diamond where his heart should be.\n    24→- **Years ago**: Bartrim marries Evelyn Steelforge (master armorer). Thorin Ghost-Eye's archer kills Evelyn; Bartrim is exiled.\n    25→- **Years ago**: Finkle Hollingsworth explores the Forest's Zenith, activates a seal, and is possessed by Ramses/Mordred.\n    26→- **~2 years before campaign**: Mordred (as Finkle) founds the Scarlet Tunic Brigade in Argos as a soul-harvesting operation, recruiting and killing adventurers. Over time, harvests 2,000 souls.\n    27→- **~2 years before campaign**: Children begin disappearing in Argos -- hag coven kidnapping 4-year-olds to brew Pestilence from their developing souls.\n    28→- **Sometime before campaign**: The Nine Hells collapse, dispersing fiends across the planes. The plane of fire comes under threat from demons. Fire Princess Ember Ashborn goes missing.\n    29→\n    30→---\n    31→\n    32→## Act 1 — Argos and the Awakening\n    33→\n    34→### Chapter 0: The Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n    35→- Party arrives in Argos and is recruited into the Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n    36→- Finkle Hollingsworth (secretly Mordred) briefs them on their mission\n    37→- Flying kobold attack during orientation\n    38→- Jorgen arrives, having lost his previous party in the sewers\n    39→- First mission assigned: clear the sewer ritual chamber\n    40→\n    41→### Chapter 1: The Sewer Ritual\n    42→- Party descends into Argos sewers; Jorgen leads them into a trap\n    43→- Blood rune button puzzle with descending ceiling\n    44→- Discovery that Stromble and Grom were priming the ritual chamber\n    45→- Jorgen revealed as complicit in leading adventurers to their deaths\n    46→- Party discovers the Ritual of Life chamber with four magical liquid streams (blood, poison, mercury, liquid souls)\n    47→- Dallor pickpockets evidence of Finkle's betrayal from Jorgen\n    48→- Ritual completed; blinding light engulfs the chamber\n    49→\n    50→### Chapter 2: The Gnome Eff'n Way"}]},"uuid":"806f1cb7-7496-4869-abef-4311e05d2d8c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:54.879Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9f957451-075f-4de5-9584-2dc1701bf2ea","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

29→### Phase 1: The Split (Chapters 42-51)\n    30→*Campaign divides into Dwarves and Tieflings tracks*\n    31→\n    32→**Chapter 42** [MEDIUM]\n    33→- Campaign splits into two parallel parties: {"parentUuid":"7a414b52-cf0e-4247-a48d-9dbf2542c1d0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6d642684aa325cba","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_0181a4DrnGxpgTUcbVDZw1R9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0152zgy6amzte8HaAe6RReFv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/tools/generate-canvas.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGenerate Obsidian .canvas files from the Mardov vault data.\n\nProduces three canvases:\n  1. relationship-map.canvas  -- PCs + NPCs with relationship edges\n  2. timeline.canvas          -- Chronological events by act\n  3. faction-web.canvas       -- Factions with member nodes and inter-faction edges\n\nUsage:\n    python3 tools/generate-canvas.py [--vault PATH]\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport math\nimport os\nimport re\nimport uuid\nimport yaml\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Utilities\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef uid():\n    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]\n\n\ndef parse_frontmatter(path):\n    \"\"\"Return (frontmatter_dict, body_str) from a markdown file with YAML frontmatter.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            text = f.read()\n    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):\n        return {}, \"\"\n    if not text.startswith(\"---\"):\n        return {}, text\n    end = text.find(\"---\", 3)\n    if end == -1:\n        return {}, text\n    try:\n        fm = yaml.safe_load(text[3:end]) or {}\n    except yaml.YAMLError:\n        fm = {}\n    body = text[end + 3:].strip()\n    return fm, body\n\n\ndef collect_md_files(directory):\n    \"\"\"Yield (filepath, frontmatter, body) for every .md in directory tree.\"\"\"\n    if not os.path.isdir(directory):\n        return\n    for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(directory):\n        for fn in sorted(files):\n            if fn.endswith(\".md\"):\n                path = os.path.join(root, fn)\n                fm, body = parse_frontmatter(path)\n                yield path, fm, body\n\n\ndef make_node(nid, ntype, x, y, w, h, color=None, **kwargs):\n    node = {\"id\": nid, \"type\": ntype, \"x\": x, \"y\": y, \"width\": w, \"height\": h}\n    if color:\n        node[\"color\"] = str(color)\n    node.update(kwargs)\n    return node\n\n\ndef make_file_node(nid, filepath, x, y, w=280, h=60, color=None):\n    return make_node(nid, \"file\", x, y, w, h, color, file=filepath)\n\n\ndef make_text_node(nid, text, x, y, w=280, h=60, color=None):\n    return make_node(nid, \"text\", x, y, w, h, color, text=text)\n\n\ndef make_edge(from_id, to_id, label=\"\"):\n    e = {\"id\": uid(), \"fromNode\": from_id, \"toNode\": to_id}\n    if label:\n        e[\"label\"] = label\n    return e\n\n\ndef write_canvas(path, nodes, edges):\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)\n    data = {\"nodes\": nodes, \"edges\": edges}\n    with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n\ndef vault_relative(abspath, vault_root):\n    \"\"\"Convert absolute path to vault-relative path for Obsidian file nodes.\"\"\"\n    return os.path.relpath(abspath, vault_root)\n\n\ndef strip_wikilinks(text):\n    \"\"\"Remove [[ ]] wikilink syntax, keeping display text.\"\"\"\n    return re.sub(r'\\[\\[([^|\\]]*\\|)?([^\\]]+)\\]\\]', r'\\2', text)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 1. Relationship Map\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nCOLOR_PC = \"4\"        # green\nCOLOR_NPC_MAJOR = \"2\" # orange\nCOLOR_NPC_MINOR = \"3\" # yellow\nCOLOR_ANTAGONIST = \"1\" # red\nCOLOR_DEITY = \"6\"     # purple\n\n# Keywords in body/frontmatter that flag antagonists\nANTAGONIST_KEYWORDS = [\n    \"vecna sworn\", \"antagonist\", \"villain\", \"evil\", \"enemy\",\n    \"necromancer\", \"lich\", \"vampire queen\", \"therzadun\",\n]\nDEITY_KEYWORDS = [\n    \"god \", \"goddess\", \"deity\", \"divine\", \"cosmic entit\", \"archdevil\",\n    \"bahamut\", \"tiamat\", \"raven queen\", \"asmodeus\", \"lolth\",\n]\n\n\ndef classify_npc(fm, body, subdir):\n    \"\"\"Return color code for an NPC based on content.\"\"\"\n    combined = (json.dumps(fm) + \" \" + body[:2000]).lower()\n    affiliation = str(fm.get(\"affiliation\", \"\")).lower()\n\n    # Check antagonist first\n    if any(kw in combined for kw in ANTAGONIST_KEYWORDS):\n        if any(kw in affiliation for kw in [\"vecna\", \"therzadun\"]):\n            return COLOR_ANTAGONIST\n        # Major NPCs with antagonist keywords but not sworn to evil\n        # could still be antagonists if they are explicitly tagged\n        role = str(fm.get(\"role\", \"\")).lower()\n        if \"antagonist\" in combined or \"villain\" in combined:\n            return COLOR_ANTAGONIST\n\n    # Deities / cosmic entities\n    if \"organizations\" in subdir:\n        # Organization files like Gods and Cosmic Entities\n        if any(kw in combined for kw in DEITY_KEYWORDS):\n            return COLOR_DEITY\n    role = str(fm.get(\"role\", \"\")).lower()\n    if any(kw in combined[:500] for kw in DEITY_KEYWORDS):\n        return COLOR_DEITY\n\n    # Major vs minor\n    if \"major\" in subdir or fm.get(\"role\") == \"npc-major\":\n        return COLOR_NPC_MAJOR\n    return COLOR_NPC_MINOR\n\n\ndef parse_relationship_pairs(rel_path):\n    \"\"\"Extract relationship pairs from the character-relationships.md file.\"\"\"\n    _, body = parse_frontmatter(rel_path)\n    pairs = []\n    # Match section headers like \"### Brigit & Daina -- \"Sisters in Arms\"\"\n    pattern = re.compile(\n        r'###\\s+(.+?)\\s+&\\s+(.+?)\\s+--\\s+\"([^\"]+)\"',\n        re.IGNORECASE,\n    )\n    for m in pattern.finditer(body):\n        name_a = m.group(1).strip()\n        name_b = m.group(2).strip()\n        label = m.group(3).strip()\n        pairs.append((name_a, name_b, label))\n\n    # Also match the brief pairings table: | Pair | Dynamic | Defining Moment |\n    table_pattern = re.compile(\n        r'^\\|\\s*(.+?)\\s+&\\s+(.+?)\\s*\\|\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\|',\n        re.MULTILINE,\n    )\n    for m in table_pattern.finditer(body):\n        name_a = m.group(1).strip()\n        name_b = m.group(2).strip()\n        label = m.group(3).strip()\n        if name_a.lower() == \"pair\":\n            continue\n        pairs.append((name_a, name_b, label))\n\n    # Zenith grouping edges from the appendix table\n    zenith_pattern = re.compile(\n        r'^\\|\\s*(Forest|Mountain|Winter|Harvest)\\s.*?\\|\\s*\\w+\\s*\\|\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\|',\n        re.MULTILINE,\n    )\n    for m in zenith_pattern.finditer(body):\n        zenith = m.group(1).strip()\n        chars_raw = m.group(2).strip()\n        chars = [c.strip() for c in chars_raw.split(\",\")]\n        # Add edges between all members of the zenith group\n        for i in range(len(chars)):\n            for j in range(i + 1, len(chars)):\n                pairs.append((chars[i], chars[j], f\"{zenith} Zenith\"))\n\n    return pairs\n\n\ndef fuzzy_match(name, candidates):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching candidate key for a character name.\"\"\"\n    name_lower = name.lower().strip()\n    # Exact match\n    for key in candidates:\n        if key.lower() == name_lower:\n            return key\n    # First-name match\n    first = name_lower.split()[0] if name_lower else \"\"\n    matches = [k for k in candidates if k.lower().startswith(first)]\n    if len(matches) == 1:\n        return matches[0]\n    # Partial match -- name contained in key or vice versa\n    for key in candidates:\n        if name_lower in key.lower() or key.lower() in name_lower:\n            return key\n    # Check without \"the\" prefix\n    for key in candidates:\n        key_clean = key.lower().replace(\"the \", \"\")\n        if name_lower.replace(\"the \", \"\") == key_clean:\n            return key\n    return None\n\n\ndef build_relationship_map(vault_root):\n    \"\"\"Generate the relationship-map.canvas.\"\"\"\n    chars_dir = os.path.join(vault_root, \"characters\")\n    rel_file = os.path.join(vault_root, \"sources\", \"lore-extraction\", \"character-relationships.md\")\n\n    # Collect PCs\n    pcs = {}\n    pc_dir = os.path.join(chars_dir, \"pcs\")\n    for path, fm, body in collect_md_files(pc_dir):\n        name = fm.get(\"name\", os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(path))[0])\n        pcs[name] = {\"path\": path, \"fm\": fm, \"body\": body}\n\n    # Collect NPCs (major, minor, organizations)\n    npcs = {}\n    for subdir in [\"npcs/major\", \"npcs/minor\", \"npcs/organizations\"]:\n        npc_dir = os.path.join(chars_dir, subdir)\n        for path, fm, body in collect_md_files(npc_dir):\n            name = fm.get(\"name\", os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(path))[0])\n            color = classify_npc(fm, body, subdir)\n            npcs[name] = {\"path\": path, \"fm\": fm, \"body\": body, \"color\": color, \"subdir\": subdir}\n\n    nodes = []\n    edges = []\n    name_to_id = {}\n\n    # Layout: PCs in a central ring\n    pc_names = sorted(pcs.keys())\n    num_pcs = len(pc_names)\n    pc_radius = 400\n    center_x, center_y = 0, 0\n\n    for i, name in enumerate(pc_names):\n        angle = (2 * math.pi * i) / max(num_pcs, 1)\n        x = center_x + int(pc_radius * math.cos(angle))\n        y = center_y + int(pc_radius * math.sin(angle))\n        nid = uid()\n        name_to_id[name] = nid\n        rel_path = vault_relative(pcs[name][\"path\"], vault_root)\n        nodes.append(make_file_node(nid, rel_path, x, y, 280, 60, COLOR_PC))\n\n    # NPCs arranged in outer rings by color/category\n    npc_names = sorted(npcs.keys())\n    # Group by color for visual clustering\n    color_groups = {}\n    for name in npc_names:\n        c = npcs[name][\"color\"]\n        color_groups.setdefault(c, []).append(name)\n\n    # Place each color group in a ring sector\n    npc_radius_base = 900\n    ring_offset = 0\n    for color_key in [COLOR_NPC_MAJOR, COLOR_ANTAGONIST, COLOR_DEITY, COLOR_NPC_MINOR]:\n        group = color_groups.get(color_key, [])\n        if not group:\n            continue\n        radius = npc_radius_base + ring_offset\n        for i, name in enumerate(group):\n            angle = (2 * math.pi * i) / max(len(group), 1) + ring_offset * 0.1\n            x = center_x + int(radius * math.cos(angle))\n            y = center_y + int(radius * math.sin(angle))\n            nid = uid()\n            name_to_id[name] = nid\n            rel_path = vault_relative(npcs[name][\"path\"], vault_root)\n            nodes.append(make_file_node(nid, rel_path, x, y, 260, 50, color_key))\n        ring_offset += 250\n\n    # Parse relationships and create edges\n    if os.path.exists(rel_file):\n        pairs = parse_relationship_pairs(rel_file)\n        all_names = set(name_to_id.keys())\n        for name_a, name_b, label in pairs:\n            # Clean wikilink syntax\n            name_a = strip_wikilinks(name_a)\n            name_b = strip_wikilinks(name_b)\n            key_a = fuzzy_match(name_a, all_names)\n            key_b = fuzzy_match(name_b, all_names)\n            if key_a and key_b and key_a != key_b:\n                id_a = name_to_id[key_a]\n                id_b = name_to_id[key_b]\n                edges.append(make_edge(id_a, id_b, label))\n\n    # Also extract relationships from PC frontmatter (affiliation -> faction NPCs)\n    # These create lighter connections between PCs and their affiliated NPCs\n    for pc_name, pc_data in pcs.items():\n        relationships = pc_data.get(\"body\", \"\")\n        # Find wikilinked names in the Relationships section\n        rel_section = re.search(r'## Relationships\\s*\\n(.*?)(?=\\n## |\\Z)', relationships, re.DOTALL)\n        if rel_section:\n            linked_names = re.findall(r'\\[\\[([^\\]|]+)', rel_section.group(1))\n            for linked in linked_names:\n                key = fuzzy_match(linked.strip(), set(name_to_id.keys()))\n                if key and key != pc_name and pc_name in name_to_id:\n                    # Avoid duplicate edges\n                    existing = {(e[\"fromNode\"], e[\"toNode\"]) for e in edges}\n                    pair = (name_to_id[pc_name], name_to_id[key])\n                    reverse = (name_to_id[key], name_to_id[pc_name])\n                    if pair not in existing and reverse not in existing:\n                        edges.append(make_edge(name_to_id[pc_name], name_to_id[key]))\n\n    # Add a title node\n    title_id = uid()\n    nodes.insert(0, make_text_node(\n        title_id,\n        \"# Mardov Relationship Map\\n\\nGreen = PC | Orange = Major NPC | \"\n        \"Yellow = Minor NPC | Red = Antagonist | Purple = Deity/Cosmic\",\n        center_x - 200, center_y - pc_radius - 200, 500, 100,\n    ))\n\n    canvas_path = os.path.join(vault_root, \"canvas\", \"relationship-map.canvas\")\n    write_canvas(canvas_path, nodes, edges)\n    return len(nodes), len(edges), canvas_path\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 2. Timeline Canvas\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nACT_COLORS = {\n    \"ancient\": \"6\",  # purple for prehistory\n    \"recent\": \"5\",   # cyan for recent past\n    \"1\": \"4\",        # green\n    \"2\": \"3\",        # yellow\n    \"3\": \"2\",        # orange\n    \"4\": \"1\",        # red\n    \"5\": \"6\",        # purple\n}\n\n\ndef parse_timeline_events(vault_root):\n    \"\"\"Parse timeline.md and extraction files into structured events.\"\"\"\n    events = []\n\n    # Primary timeline\n    timeline_path = os.path.join(vault_root, \"lore\", \"timeline.md\")\n    if os.path.exists(timeline_path):\n        _, body = parse_frontmatter(timeline_path)\n\n        # Parse the structured sections\n        current_act = \"ancient\"\n        current_section = \"\"\n\n        for line in body.split(\"\\n\"):\n            line = line.strip()\n\n            # Detect act headers\n            if re.match(r'^## Ancient History', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"ancient\"\n                current_section = \"Ancient History\"\n            elif re.match(r'^## Recent Past', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"recent\"\n                current_section = \"Recent Past\"\n            elif re.match(r'^### Act 1', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"1\"\n                current_section = \"Act 1: The Awakening\"\n            elif re.match(r'^### Act 2', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"2\"\n                current_section = \"Act 2: The Voyage\"\n            elif re.match(r'^### Act 3', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"3\"\n                current_section = \"Act 3: The Escalation\"\n            elif re.match(r'^### Act 4', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"4\"\n                current_section = \"Act 4: The Endgame\"\n            elif re.match(r'^## Prophesied', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"5\"\n                current_section = \"Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\"\n            elif re.match(r'^## The True History', line, re.I):\n                current_act = \"ancient\"\n                current_section = \"The True History\"\n\n            # Parse bullet-point events\n            m = re.match(r'^-\\s+(.+)', line)\n            if m and current_act in (\"ancient\", \"recent\"):\n                event_text = strip_wikilinks(m.group(1).strip())\n                events.append({\n                    \"act\": current_act,\n                    \"section\": current_section,\n                    \"text\": event_text,\n                    \"chapter\": None,\n                })\n\n            # Parse table-row events: | Ch. | Event |\n            m = re.match(r'^\\|\\s*(\\d+\\S*)\\s*\\|\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\|', line)\n            if m:\n                ch = m.group(1).strip()\n                event_text = strip_wikilinks(m.group(2).strip())\n                if event_text.lower() != \"event\":  # skip header\n                    events.append({\n                        \"act\": current_act,\n                        \"section\": current_section,\n                        \"text\": f\"Ch. {ch}: {event_text}\",\n                        \"chapter\": ch,\n                    })\n\n    return events\n\n\ndef build_timeline(vault_root):\n    \"\"\"Generate the timeline.canvas.\"\"\"\n    events = parse_timeline_events(vault_root)\n\n    nodes = []\n    edges = []\n\n    # Layout: horizontal timeline, grouped by act\n    # Each act gets a column, events stack vertically within\n    act_order = [\"ancient\", \"recent\", \"1\", \"2\", \"3\", \"4\", \"5\"]\n    act_labels = {\n        \"ancient\": \"Ancient History\",\n        \"recent\": \"Recent Past\",\n        \"1\": \"Act 1: The Awakening\\n(Ch. 0-13)\",\n        \"2\": \"Act 2: The Voyage\\n(Ch. 14-40)\",\n        \"3\": \"Act 3: The Escalation\\n(Ch. 41-86)\",\n        \"4\": \"Act 4: The Endgame\\n(Ch. 87-136)\",\n        \"5\": \"Prophesied:\\nGard'Daemon\",\n    }\n\n    col_width = 400\n    node_height = 70\n    node_width = 350\n    y_spacing = 90\n    x_start = 0\n\n    # Group events by act\n    by_act = {}\n    for e in events:\n        by_act.setdefault(e[\"act\"], []).append(e)\n\n    prev_last_id = None\n\n    for col_idx, act_key in enumerate(act_order):\n        x = x_start + col_idx * col_width\n        color = ACT_COLORS.get(act_key, \"5\")\n\n        # Act header node\n        header_id = uid()\n        nodes.append(make_text_node(\n            header_id,\n            f\"# {act_labels.get(act_key, act_key)}\",\n            x, -120, node_width, 80, color,\n        ))\n\n        act_events = by_act.get(act_key, [])\n        last_id = header_id\n\n        for i, event in enumerate(act_events):\n            y = i * y_spacing\n            nid = uid()\n            text = event[\"text\"]\n            # Truncate very long events for canvas readability\n            if len(text) > 200:\n                text = text[:197] + \"...\"\n            nodes.append(make_text_node(nid, text, x, y, node_width, node_height, color))\n            # Chain events within the act\n            edges.append(make_edge(last_id, nid))\n            last_id = nid\n\n        # Connect act columns\n        if prev_last_id:\n            edges.append(make_edge(prev_last_id, header_id, \"-->\"))\n        prev_last_id = last_id\n\n    # Title node\n    title_id = uid()\n    nodes.insert(0, make_text_node(\n        title_id,\n        \"# Mardov Campaign Timeline\\n\\nLeft to right: Ancient -> Endgame\\n\"\n        \"Green=Act1 | Yellow=Act2 | Orange=Act3 | Red=Act4 | Purple=Ancient/Prophesied | Cyan=Recent\",\n        x_start - 50, -250, 600, 100,\n    ))\n\n    canvas_path = os.path.join(vault_root, \"canvas\", \"timeline.canvas\")\n    write_canvas(canvas_path, nodes, edges)\n    return len(nodes), len(edges), canvas_path\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 3. Faction Web\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nFACTION_CATEGORY_COLORS = {\n    \"planar\": \"1\",       # red -- otherworldly threats\n    \"criminal\": \"1\",     # red\n    \"military\": \"2\",     # orange\n    \"organization\": \"3\", # yellow\n    \"political\": \"5\",    # cyan\n    \"religious\": \"6\",    # purple\n}\n\n\ndef extract_faction_members(body):\n    \"\"\"Extract member names from faction body text (tables, lists).\"\"\"\n    members = []\n    # Match table rows: | Name | Role |\n    for m in re.finditer(r'^\\|\\s*\\[\\[([^\\]|]+)', body, re.MULTILINE):\n        members.append(m.group(1).strip())\n    # Also match plain names in tables (no wikilinks)\n    for m in re.finditer(r'^\\|\\s*([A-Z][a-z]+ [A-Z][a-z]+(?:\\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)?)\\s*\\|', body, re.MULTILINE):\n        name = m.group(1).strip()\n        if name not in (\"Name Role\", \"Name Notes\") and not name.startswith(\"Name\"):\n            members.append(name)\n    # Match list items with wikilinks\n    for m in re.finditer(r'^-\\s+\\*?\\*?\\[\\[([^\\]|]+)', body, re.MULTILINE):\n        members.append(m.group(1).strip())\n    # Deduplicate\n    seen = set()\n    unique = []\n    for name in members:\n        if name.lower() not in seen:\n            seen.add(name.lower())\n            unique.append(name)\n    return unique\n\n\ndef extract_inter_faction_relations(body, faction_name):\n    \"\"\"Look for references to other factions in the body.\"\"\"\n    relations = []\n    # Look for Allies/Enemies sections\n    allies_section = re.search(r'##\\s*Allies.*?\\n(.*?)(?=\\n## |\\Z)', body, re.DOTALL | re.I)\n    enemies_section = re.search(r'##\\s*Enemies.*?\\n(.*?)(?=\\n## |\\Z)', body, re.DOTALL | re.I)\n\n    if allies_section:\n        for m in re.finditer(r'\\[\\[([^\\]|]+)', allies_section.group(1)):\n            relations.append((m.group(1).strip(), \"allied\"))\n    if enemies_section:\n        for m in re.finditer(r'\\[\\[([^\\]|]+)', enemies_section.group(1)):\n            relations.append((m.group(1).strip(), \"hostile\"))\n\n    return relations\n\n\ndef build_faction_web(vault_root):\n    \"\"\"Generate the faction-web.canvas.\"\"\"\n    factions_dir = os.path.join(vault_root, \"factions\")\n\n    factions = {}\n    for path, fm, body in collect_md_files(factions_dir):\n        name = fm.get(\"name\", os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(path))[0])\n        category = fm.get(\"category\", \"organization\")\n        color = FACTION_CATEGORY_COLORS.get(category, \"3\")\n        members = extract_faction_members(body)\n        relations = extract_inter_faction_relations(body, name)\n        status = fm.get(\"status\", \"active\")\n        leader = strip_wikilinks(str(fm.get(\"leader\", \"\")))\n\n        factions[name] = {\n            \"path\": path,\n            \"fm\": fm,\n            \"body\": body,\n            \"color\": color,\n            \"category\": category,\n            \"members\": members,\n            \"relations\": relations,\n            \"status\": status,\n            \"leader\": leader,\n        }\n\n    nodes = []\n    edges = []\n    name_to_id = {}\n    member_to_id = {}  # track member nodes for cross-faction links\n\n    # Layout: factions in a large circle, members as satellites\n    faction_names = sorted(factions.keys())\n    num_factions = len(faction_names)\n    faction_radius = 1200\n    center_x, center_y = 0, 0\n\n    for fi, faction_name in enumerate(faction_names):\n        fdata = factions[faction_name]\n        angle = (2 * math.pi * fi) / max(num_factions, 1)\n        fx = center_x + int(faction_radius * math.cos(angle))\n        fy = center_y + int(faction_radius * math.sin(angle))\n\n        # Faction node (larger)\n        fid = uid()\n        name_to_id[faction_name] = fid\n        rel_path = vault_relative(fdata[\"path\"], vault_root)\n        nodes.append(make_file_node(fid, rel_path, fx, fy, 320, 80, fdata[\"color\"]))\n\n        # Leader subtitle node\n        if fdata[\"leader\"]:\n            lid = uid()\n            nodes.append(make_text_node(\n                lid, f\"Leader: {fdata['leader']}\",\n                fx + 10, fy + 90, 300, 40, fdata[\"color\"],\n            ))\n            edges.append(make_edge(fid, lid, \"leads\"))\n\n        # Member satellite nodes\n        members = fdata[\"members\"][:20]  # cap to avoid clutter\n        member_radius = 200 + len(members) * 15\n        for mi, member_name in enumerate(members):\n            m_angle = angle + (2 * math.pi * mi) / max(len(members), 1) * 0.6 - 0.3\n            mx = fx + int(member_radius * math.cos(m_angle))\n            my = fy + int(member_radius * math.sin(m_angle))\n\n            mid = uid()\n            # Check if this member has a character file in the vault\n            member_file = None\n            for subdir in [\"characters/pcs\", \"characters/npcs/major\",\n                           \"characters/npcs/minor\", \"characters/npcs/organizations\"]:\n                candidate = os.path.join(vault_root, subdir, f\"{member_name}.md\")\n                if os.path.exists(candidate):\n                    member_file = candidate\n                    break\n\n            if member_file:\n                nodes.append(make_file_node(\n                    mid, vault_relative(member_file, vault_root),\n                    mx, my, 250, 50,\n                ))\n            else:\n                nodes.append(make_text_node(mid, member_name, mx, my, 250, 50))\n\n            edges.append(make_edge(fid, mid, \"member\"))\n\n            # Track for cross-faction edges\n            member_key = member_name.lower()\n            if member_key not in member_to_id:\n                member_to_id[member_key] = mid\n            else:\n                # Member appears in multiple factions -- link them\n                edges.append(make_edge(member_to_id[member_key], mid, \"also in\"))\n\n    # Inter-faction relationship edges\n    for faction_name, fdata in factions.items():\n        if faction_name not in name_to_id:\n            continue\n        for target_name, relation_type in fdata[\"relations\"]:\n            # Try to match target to a faction\n            key = fuzzy_match(target_name, set(name_to_id.keys()))\n            if key and key != faction_name:\n                src = name_to_id[faction_name]\n                dst = name_to_id[key]\n                # Avoid duplicate edges\n                existing = {(e[\"fromNode\"], e[\"toNode\"], e.get(\"label\", \"\"))\n                            for e in edges}\n                if (src, dst, relation_type) not in existing and \\\n                   (dst, src, relation_type) not in existing:\n                    edges.append(make_edge(src, dst, relation_type))\n\n    # Also scan character files for faction affiliations to create cross-links\n    chars_dir = os.path.join(vault_root, \"characters\")\n    for path, fm, body in collect_md_files(chars_dir):\n        affiliation = str(fm.get(\"affiliation\", \"\"))\n        char_name = fm.get(\"name\", \"\")\n        if not affiliation or not char_name:\n            continue\n        # Find which factions this character is affiliated with\n        affiliated_factions = []\n        for fname in name_to_id:\n            if fname.lower() in affiliation.lower():\n                affiliated_factions.append(fname)\n        # If character is in multiple factions, link those factions\n        for i in range(len(affiliated_factions)):\n            for j in range(i + 1, len(affiliated_factions)):\n                src = name_to_id[affiliated_factions[i]]\n                dst = name_to_id[affiliated_factions[j]]\n                existing_pairs = {(e[\"fromNode\"], e[\"toNode\"]) for e in edges}\n                if (src, dst) not in existing_pairs and (dst, src) not in existing_pairs:\n                    edges.append(make_edge(src, dst, f\"shared: {char_name}\"))\n\n    # Title node\n    title_id = uid()\n    nodes.insert(0, make_text_node(\n        title_id,\n        \"# Mardov Faction Web\\n\\nRed = Planar/Criminal | Orange = Military | \"\n        \"Yellow = Organization | Cyan = Political | Purple = Religious\",\n        center_x - 300, center_y - faction_radius - 200, 600, 100,\n    ))\n\n    canvas_path = os.path.join(vault_root, \"canvas\", \"faction-web.canvas\")\n    write_canvas(canvas_path, nodes, edges)\n    return len(nodes), len(edges), canvas_path\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Generate Obsidian canvases from Mardov vault\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--vault\",\n        default=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),\n                             \"mardov-vault\"),\n        help=\"Path to the Mardov vault root\",\n    )\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    vault_root = os.path.abspath(args.vault)\n\n    if not os.path.isdir(vault_root):\n        print(f\"ERROR: vault directory not found: {vault_root}\")\n        return 1\n\n    print(f\"Vault: {vault_root}\")\n    print()\n\n    # 1. Relationship Map\n    print(\"=== Generating relationship-map.canvas ===\")\n    n_nodes, n_edges, path = build_relationship_map(vault_root)\n    print(f\"  Nodes: {n_nodes}  |  Edges: {n_edges}\")\n    print(f\"  Written: {path}\")\n    print()\n\n    # 2. Timeline\n    print(\"=== Generating timeline.canvas ===\")\n    n_nodes, n_edges, path = build_timeline(vault_root)\n    print(f\"  Nodes: {n_nodes}  |  Edges: {n_edges}\")\n    print(f\"  Written: {path}\")\n    print()\n\n    # 3. Faction Web\n    print(\"=== Generating faction-web.canvas ===\")\n    n_nodes, n_edges, path = build_faction_web(vault_root)\n    print(f\"  Nodes: {n_nodes}  |  Edges: {n_edges}\")\n    print(f\"  Written: {path}\")\n    print()\n\n    print(\"Done. Open the canvas/ directory in Obsidian to view.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3356,"cache_read_input_tokens":58085,"output_tokens":8674,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3356},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9gcmgBbU7pifrc557Bp","type":"assistant","uuid":"b6852423-7066-43cb-9526-182e5e659884","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:34:53.026Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
